Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 3.11.3.

  • fix(render): apply crop to each clip before joining (#590)

Closes: #551, #552

Model catalog fix

Highlights

  • Newly released models such as GPT-6.2 Sol appear in the model picker even when live catalog updates arrive while an agent is busy

Fixes

  • Update lettre, anyhow, event-listener, and tokio for new RustSec advisories

cua-driver-rs-v0.53.2cua-driver-rs: v0.53.2 Prerelease2026-11-18T13:06:54Z

Cua Driver 0.53.2

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • add started_at_unix_ms to recording session.json. (#8118) Thanks @Iflaqbhat; reported by @methanoya.

Contributors

Thanks to @Iflaqbhat, @methanoya for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

cua-driver-rs-v0.53.1cua-driver-rs: v0.53.1 Prerelease2026-11-18T12:25:45Z

Cua Driver 0.53.1

Summary

This release includes 4 fixes.

Fixes

  • report macOS AX enumeration counts in ax_window_unresolved. (#8096) Thanks @lorenzozanee; reported by @JayLewisLLC.
  • publish display-only rows as observations so verify_state can read them. (#7566)
  • run a background pixel-targeted menu key equivalent on macOS. (#8081) reported by @howardxie-dev.
  • explain session-scoped snapshots in the stale token error. (#8110) Thanks @Alx8g.

Contributors

Thanks to @Alx8g, @howardxie-dev, @JayLewisLLC, @lorenzozanee for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Cua Driver 0.53.1-nightly.20261118.44236707997 (nightly)

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

Source

  • Commit: dde4a4b64a49629ef41fb0faf19c45a178e0b08a
  • Changes: nightly-cua-driver-rs-v0.52.12-nightly.20261117.44016600295…dde4a4b64a49

Changes

  • Fix: run one macOS AppKit E2E cell alone from appkit-cells.tsv. (#7229)
  • Fix: refuse trusted browser ref clicks whose point misses the ref. (#7512)
  • Feat: background input for GTK 3 apps on macOS through the AppKit activation event. (#7950)
  • Fix: keep the agent pointer and badge visible and require them in the recorded video. (#7569)
  • Fix: ship LICENSE files in Python distributions. (#8056) Thanks @cnYui; reported by @PreetamMatta.

Contributors

Thanks to @cnYui, @PreetamMatta for contributing to or reporting changes in this nightly.

CUA_DRIVER_RS_VERSION=nightly-cua-driver-rs-v0.53.1-nightly.20261118.44236707997 /bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v0.9.66 Stable2026-11-18T12:43:08Z

HyperFrames v0.9.66

Released on 2026-11-18.

Studio's sealed preview now takes property edits, crops, resizes and plays slideshow scenes. A preview no longer sits in Buffering when a clip stops downloading, and the player can carry your own branding.

Features

  • Player: Add customizable branding to the preview player (a9b4745f1, #7718).

Fixes

  • AWS Lambda: Match the shell to its own pin, ship only hashed files, refuse the shell ZIP in CDK and SAM (61d65e717, #8173).
  • AWS Lambda: Pin the shell ZIP route, gate ZIP headroom, match arm64 fonts to x86 (ed3f05a05, #8170).
  • Studio: Property panel style and attribute edits show in a sealed preview (b525686e7, #8149).
  • Compiler: Rebase composition asset paths from the file read and charge what they add (bb55c1ea7, #8166).
  • CLI: Figma commands print manifest and folder text with visible escapes (420f14d15, #8126).
  • CLI: Show control characters in printed names as visible escapes (3e788fb15, #8008).
  • Skills: The launcher's floor is 0.9.65, the oldest release Desktop ships (140b70140, #8159).
  • AWS Lambda: Chrome-headless-shell 158 in the arm64 video image, sparticuz 153 in the x86 ZIP (81d73d227, #8155).
  • Producer: Inline a local font only while the page stays within its size limit (3b79b1858, #8152).
  • Studio: A sealed preview's element reads are bounded, and a frame with no agent answers at once (e623031f3, #8055).
  • Core: Each editing client takes its own port, so a sealed preview answers Studio again (e95cdf7d7, #8154).
  • Core: Keep SVG id renaming linear in the page size and within the render size limit (5bd3e3242, #8147).
  • Studio: Check every file a folder move or delete takes along (7ea012953, #8143).
  • Studio: A sealed preview takes its film's size from the player (d574ded1e, #7967).
  • Core: Keep the styles of a composition whose id contains $ patterns (12a2f83c4, #8141).
  • Compiler: Bound composition variable styles and variable media sources (c6e3f30be, #8144).
  • Core: A clip that stops downloading no longer holds the preview in Buffering forever (01331014d, #8138).
  • Player: Keep the video playing when you jump to another moment (3ee095bde, #7719).
  • CLI: Run global updates from the home directory (e99cd75b4, #8131).
  • Media Use: Escape control characters in the remaining resolve and cube-validate lines (9a096d72f, #8059).
  • Media: Keep each index.md row on one line and show control characters as escapes (ebddc4248, #8127).
  • CLI: Tests read opencv.js from a CI cache, never a CDN mid-test (e6cee094b, #7968).
  • Studio: Crop and scale resize work in a sealed preview (c569417bb, #8005).
  • Core: Treat files package managers run by default as tool-trusted (5f06e1b0a, #8129).
  • Skills: The launcher never starts a CLI older than its security floor (63c8f8ef6, #8125).
  • CLI: Say when check's face pass stopped a source at its time limit (59fbb22eb, #8122).
  • CLI: Media-use scripts read the host's kept record copy as the CLI does (c27adb35f, #8119).
  • Studio: Slideshow scenes, block stacking, drill-down and shortcuts work in a sealed preview (80bf6a9dd, #8118).
  • CLI: A rename keeps the received-files record listing the moved file (cb2b51525, #8112).

Docs & Examples

  • Ci: Say the npm-publish environment needs a reviewer's approval (d94aa5e30, #8123).

Internal

  • Producer: The missing-file render detail added in this cycle was taken out again before release, and returns in a later one (cfea8cfff, #7955, eb7dc14e8, #8146, 94d06663b, #8174).
  • Player: One hung Safari run no longer fails every Safari run after it (9acf26868, #8169).
  • Studio: Share one sealed preview frame that delivers messages like a browser (640cb3792, #8165).
  • Chrome: Pin chrome-headless-shell 158.0.8127.36 wherever the repo installs it (793052502, #8145).
  • Lint: Fail when a RegExp is built from text that is not escaped (dcdd34071, #8128).

Full changelog

v0.9.65 Stable2026-11-18T00:05:35Z

HyperFrames v0.9.65

Released on 2026-11-17.

Studio's sealed preview now draws motion paths, reads keyframes and keeps a moved layer in place when you add a keyframe. Studio's error and analytics events no longer carry your error text, file paths or selectors.

Features

  • Studio Server: Let a host count the requests the API refuses with 403 (078e54d00, #8049).

Fixes

  • Studio: Error events send a class, a shape and Studio's own frames, not the error's words (195aa2f0c, #8114).
  • Studio: Add keyframe on a sealed preview keeps a moved layer in place (ce3decb6b, #8117).
  • CLI: Check's face pass reads only media the page may load (c4a4167aa, #8116).
  • Studio: A sealed preview moves and saves a layer its animation moves (7a9d78e69, #8105).
  • Studio: Analytics send path and selector facts, not the paths and selectors (d87285387, #8109).
  • Studio: The motion path draws and can be created in a sealed preview (373d987e3, #8108).
  • Skills: Select standalone CLI releases through the launcher (414adffb8, #8102).
  • Figma: Write .media and component outputs without following links (a765c9379, #8111).
  • Studio: The edit bench waits out the preview swap once a plain element gains keyframes (ba62d7748, #8113).
  • CLI: Windows Chrome cleanup and process reads no longer pass PowerShell command text (b930c4d2d, #8085).
  • Release: The no-account pusher check reads its file directly, with no pipe (740796888, #8095).
  • Producer: Bound the static media selector check by the page it walks (435dd0e68, #8091).
  • Studio: A selected element stays drawn and draggable after a sealed preview reloads (1bf629c1d, #8081).
  • Studio: A click after two quick seeks adds the keyframe in a sealed preview (93622154e, #8103).
  • Release: List the MCP server as com.heygen/hyperframes, signed by heygen.com's key (88ba1b4d7, #8106).
  • Media Use: Audio scripts judge a received .env by the CLI's rule (bc5259d2d, #8086).
  • Media Use: Write local generator output into private per-run folders (ac33c1f2b, #8030).
  • Studio: GSAP keyframe and value reads work in a sealed preview (133965e4f, #8003).
  • CLI: Keep files received that a host's kept record still lists (183bf2a41, #8100).
  • CLI: Say a listed received file is gone, and drop a media file's line on rename (4a59fef2d, #8098).

Catalog

  • Watch the MCP registry hourly for HeyGen listings server.json never held (0b0108f2f, #8107).
  • Registry: Use the installed launcher in block guides (1c99314a6, #8101).

Internal

  • Github: Secret scanning skips the generated skills manifest (06578d941, #8115).
  • Studio: The GSAP reply check shares the frame record and handle checks (53a493edc, #8104).

Full changelog

v0.9.64 Stable2026-11-17T18:56:38Z

HyperFrames v0.9.64

Released on 2026-11-17.

Hover and thumbnail captures no longer wait on a 4K clip's preview copy. In a sealed preview, scrubbing plays the music track and timeline rows know their element. hyperframes check finds text over a face. Studio's proxy refuses requests with no same-site signal. A stable release now needs a second maintainer besides its approver.

Features

  • CLI: check finds text over a face, measure cuts the names of resumed takes, and measure reports hands (820d63d7f, #8074).

Fixes

  • Core: Hover and thumbnail captures never wait for a 4K clip's preview copy (f1f8ca31c, #8079).
  • Studio: Timeline rows in a sealed preview know their element, source file and audio group (cda94eb0f, #8078).
  • Studio: Scrubbing the playhead plays the music track in a sealed preview (a56fa2caf, #8082).
  • CLI, Studio Server: Refuse ?hf-proxy when a request carries no same-site signal (4bea3e79c, #8066).
  • CLI: A project .env provider key prints a note when it is used (90de9b619, #8056).
  • CLI: Escape control characters in the media-use adopt skip line (2749aafb3, #8087).
  • Core, Lint, Parsers: Narrow the private-file rule for hidden files, build output and lint (f5ccfcc96, #7990).
  • Lint: Derive the root duration with the parser's timing reader (401991669, #8032).
  • Lint: Read a sub-composition mount's timing through the parser (c84c30fd8, #8037).
  • Core: Drop motion-blur state on __hfRuntimeTeardown (9797faea0, #7996).
  • CLI: Keep a deleted received page listed in the received-files record (e099c7d0c, #8090).

Docs & Examples

  • Docs: Desktop exports on your computer are unlimited on every plan (94cc7558e, #8093).

Internal

  • GitHub: Workflows, actions and publish scripts need a maintainer's review (4f2eaaac4, #8092).
  • Publish: Count every pusher of a release, need a second maintainer, and date approvals (b22ca1a58, #8076).

v0.9.63 Stable2026-11-17T16:24:41Z

HyperFrames v0.9.63

Released on 2026-11-17.

Studio's sealed preview shows audio meters and audio groups, a layer reorder is one undo step again, and text films start sooner. No HyperFrames server serves a project file as a service worker's script. This release also carries every change listed for 0.9.62. That version never reached npm: the merge queue landed it in one push with another PR, and the publish read only the push's last commit. Since #8077 a release publishes from its own commit in the push.

Features

  • Core: The preview can stream an element's geometry to the editor every frame (f9c5ed07b, #8052).

Performance

  • Engine: Check still frames on several pages at once so text films start sooner (8ec97da01, #7997).
  • Producer: Frames start drawing while the audio mix finishes (0be1a0719, #7985).

Fixes

  • CLI, Producer, Inspector: Render, capture and inspector servers never serve a service worker's script (72925380c, #8073).
  • Studio: A layer reorder is one undo step again (df31ce2b1, #8072).
  • Studio: Audio meters and audio groups work in a sealed preview (93a4d29c0, #8011).
  • Studio: Adding a track no longer remounts every clip on the timeline (b064e40e1, #8068).
  • Studio Server: A project file is never served as a service worker's script (b4acc1a1d, #8064).
  • Studio: A service worker a project left behind is gone before a preview frame loads (7a0bec682, #8067).
  • Core: A held video picture is drawn through a VideoFrame closed after the draw (43e5b7cec, #7971).
  • Producer: Leave outside links out of site listings by default and warn SDK callers (fbb483852, #8065).

Internal

  • Publish: A release merged in one push with other PRs publishes at its own commit (9b565c8e0, #8077).
  • Publish: Publish to npm with the job's OIDC token, not a stored token (c106793f7, #8070).
  • Core: The reloaded-video playhead test builds a smaller film so busy CI never times it out (34f98e6a0, #8069).
  • Studio: The edit bench reads a sealed preview through its editing port (5f8b7a6ee, #8010).

Full changelog

Bug Fixes

  • 2fd85731c (#16526 by @lazerg) MimeType::parse now uses the file extension for text types like .js, .css and .svg instead of sniffing the content, so a script that contains %PDF near its start is no longer served as application/pdf.

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.77 Stable2026-11-18T11:56:28Z

On Windows, a busy sign-in lock now names the process holding it. omo login, whoami and logout used to report the holder as exited or unreadable whenever it was still running, so the message gave no pid and no fix. They now name its pid and image (read with tasklist), the lock path, and taskkill /PID <pid> /F as the way to end it. (#14118, refs #14001)

Changed

  • GPT lanes (ultrabrain, deep-high, deep-low, unspecified-low), the plan reviewer and both Obsessive profiles lead with GPT-6.2 Astra and GPT-6.2 Sol. GPT-6 Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol stay right behind at the same effort, so an engine or provider without 6.2 (including GitHub Copilot) keeps the models it ran before. GPT-6.2 ids get the low..max effort ladder, and none and minimal are sent as low. (#14126) (#14149)
  • OmO now runs on senpi 2026.11.18 (from 2026.11.15-4), the release that carries the GPT-6.2 Astra and Sol catalog rows and the gpt-6.2 prompt preset. (#14126) (#14149)
  • Thread tool rows in the TUI now read like a status line instead of a JSON dump. Each thread_* tool shows one call line (the verb, the target thread's name or short id, and the message's first line) and one outcome line with a few short details: -> planner follow-up delivered (turn 306), planner: 12 messages (latest: ...), 3 threads in this workspace with the first few threads, created scratch. Delivery ids, message sequence numbers and full uuids stay out of the collapsed row; a failure shows its code, message and next step; the expanded view (ctrl+o) adds the full result. What the model receives is unchanged. (#12997) (#14141)

Fixed

  • A thread_send to a busy thread on another device that was queued there as a follow-up now answers queued with its delivery_id and effective_mode. It used to answer idempotency_uncertain ("accepted the message but did not report where it waits") even though the message was delivered and answered. The receiving engine's queued admission is its receipt, and its pending-message count does not include admitted deliveries. (#14013, fixes #14012) (#14141)
  • A reply to a peer on another device no longer answers not_found when the delivery that brought the peer's message carries no device attestation (an rpc:local/<id> or bare delivery id). If this device holds no session by that id or stated name, the reply is looked up account-wide. It is delivered only to the session the sender's durable id names; another match is reply_target_mismatch, and an id that more than one device lists is ambiguous_target with nothing sent. (#14052) (#14141)
  • thread_create with a prompt gives the new thread that prompt as its own first user message, with the creator's authority, instead of a relayed peer message under the gateway header. A later thread_send from any session, the creator included, keeps the relay header. (#14058) (#14141)
  • omo -p exits again after the run calls a thread tool. The device-service connection a thread tool opened is now closed when the session shuts down, so it no longer keeps a finished print-mode run alive. (#14062) (#14141)
  • The thread tools' descriptions now name each tool both directly and in its eval form (for example "thread_list (tool.thread_list() in an eval cell)"), so they read correctly in a session that has no eval tool, where the thread tools are ordinary direct tools. (#14070) (#14141)
  • thread_list no longer reports the sessions of a device that is offline or did not answer as live. Their rows come from the relay's cached snapshot and are now listed resumable; opening one with thread_create asks that device to reopen it, and answers device_offline while the device stays offline. A listed thread with an empty title shows its folder's name instead of an empty name; that label is for display only and is not an address. (#14084) (#14141)
  • thread_read and the thread control tools no longer answer not_found for a session that another device of the account holds but runs nowhere, when it is addressed by session id. thread_read, thread_interrupt, thread_rename, thread_set_model and thread_set_reasoning answer not_resumable, and thread_close answers closed: false with reason not_live, as on the device that holds it. A name still answers not_found there, and thread_read does not read that device's transcript. (#14087) (#14141)
  • After a sign-in refresh, short commands such as omo account whoami no longer stay running for up to 30 seconds while omo binds the device to this computer: that step now stops within its 5-second budget, and once the budget runs out it changes nothing and prints nothing. (#14090)
  • A thread tool call that stays on this device no longer asks for its own approval inside an eval cell you already approved. Listing, reading, creating, renaming, closing or messaging a local thread from one cell now asks once (for the cell) instead of once per call. A call that may change another device still asks. Local thread tools work the same signed out as signed in. (#14082)
  • thread_create on this device no longer leaves a prompt-less thread when the connection closes after the host made the session. The create finds the thread it made and answers it, and a retry under the same idempotency_key answers the same thread and admits the prompt exactly once. A retry that cannot find that thread here (a create on another device, or a host that ignored the derived id) still answers idempotency_uncertain. (#14066)
  • On Linux, a locked Secret Service that unlocks without a prompt (for example a headless gnome-keyring with an empty-password login collection) no longer blocks the OmO sign-in: omo asks it to unlock first and reports it locked only when unlocking needs a password or prompt. (#14040)
  • Cold wake: the OmO session host service no longer restarts itself every 30 seconds on Linux and macOS. It starts the host once at login, on every omo remote bootstrap, and again whenever another device wakes a session here; a start that hangs ends after 5 minutes. omo remote status shows it running while its host is up, and unknown when the host cannot be read (#13668). (#13669)

Removed

  • A session that calls one of the removed chat-binding tools (thread_bind, thread_unbind, thread_rebind, thread_bindings) now gets a plain unknown-tool answer; the hint about them is gone. (#14042) (#14141)

Added

  • omo account store enable keeps the sign-in in OmO's own keychain on a Mac whose login keychain stays locked, for example when another macOS user holds the screen or omo runs over SSH. The store is opt-in. disable moves the sign-in back, and status reports which store is in use. omo never changes the keychain search list or the default keychain, and never falls back between stores. (#14033)

Note: 5.1.76 was tagged but never published to npm. Its publish was held on Windows platform-CI reds, which are now fixed. 5.1.77 is the first published release since 5.1.75 and includes everything that was in 5.1.76.

Highlights

  • GPT-6.2 Astra / Sol routing: GPT lanes, Obsessive profiles and plan-reviewer lead with GPT-6.2, with GPT-6 models behind them; the bundled engine is senpi 2026.11.18, which adds the GPT-6.2 catalog, prompt preset and defaults (#14149, #14126).
  • Thread tools: thread_create survives a lost open reply and retries exactly once (#14060, #14066). It names a device by display name, id or unique id prefix (#14072). Reopening an exited thread by id answers that thread (#14049, #14063). Signed-out callers get no approval storm (#14082). Permission-review fixes (#14089). Thread SDK version note (#14088).
  • Windows credential lock: the lock holder is named on Windows, so a held lock no longer hangs (#14118, fixes #14154). Keychain-doctor Windows fix (#14168).
  • Dedicated keychain: OmO keeps its own keychain (#14033, #13116). Removing an item from another binary is fixed (#14102).
  • Memory sync: the sync engine now declares the recovery-phrase capability on every request, ahead of recovery-phrase support (fixes #13691).

Known test reds still tracked: #14120.

stablyai/orca ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v1.4.263 Stable2026-11-18T11:02:11Z

Previous release: 1.4.262 — Faster chat switching, lower memory use and git blame.

Lighter chat startup, Hermes in Chat & browser extension side panels ✨

Reopen workspaces full of chats with less memory use and less startup work. Restored chat interfaces load when first shown, and Orca no longer rewrites every saved conversation at launch. Opened chats keep their drafts and scroll position when you return.

  • 💬 Continue Hermes conversations in Chat: Start Hermes from the New Tab menu, or choose Resume in New Chat on a Session History entry to continue a terminal conversation in its original workspace and profile.
  • 🌐 Keep extensions beside your page: Chrome extensions that use side panels can now open in a resizable panel in Orca's browser. Click the extension's toolbar button to use its panel while browsing; extension popups also accept Paste, Copy and Select All.
  • 🖥️ Reach your remote work: Managed SSH ports now have a Forward button. On Windows, projects opened through a mapped WSL drive letter can move onto their distro's WSL host.
  • ⌨️ Keep terminal output in view after a restart: On macOS and Linux, a terminal whose shell stopped can restore its saved output with the new prompt below it. Windows keeps its existing restore behavior.

Highlights

  • Connect Linear from the command line: orca linear connect accepts an API key through stdin and connects the host you target, including a headless server. Each host keeps its own connection. Linear issue and project lists now return all results when you omit --limit.
  • Set up agents on Windows: Claude and Codex chats offer Install when the agent is missing, then Check again to detect the installation. orca agent installed also lists enabled agent CLIs on the current host for scripts and launch setup.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Hermes: Chat requires Hermes 0.23.0 or later on the machine running the workspace. Very long imports show recent messages while Hermes retains the full conversation. Chat is unavailable for SSH workspaces; a paired Orca server supports it. The first chat after Hermes' gateway stops can take a minute to start.
  • Browser identity: Native is now the default, addressing the Cloudflare verification loop seen with the previous default. Explicit identity choices are preserved; change yours in Settings → Browser → Browser identity.
  • Phone updates: The phone UI changes listed below require a corresponding mobile build. This desktop update does not update Android or iOS apps.

Known issues

  • Sleep workspace instead: The option can fail when another tab in that workspace is running a process.
  • orca agent installed --worktree: The command errors on SSH and paired-server workspaces.

What's Changed

📱 Phone & mobile

Phone source changes for agent launch choices, text size, quoted replies, pairing and connections. These require a corresponding mobile build.

v1.4.262 Stable2026-11-17T23:20:49Z

Previous release: 1.4.261: Major performance improvements, Cursor Chat and a customizable + menu.

Faster chat switching, lower memory use & git blame ✨

Keep more conversations open with less memory on macOS and Linux, and move between chats with less work happening in hidden views. This update also helps terminal sessions pick up where you left off and puts line history directly in the editor.

  • ⚡ Work across many chats: Chat UI uses lighter helpers on macOS and Linux. Hidden chat views pause their updates while retaining your draft, reading position and editor state for your return; agents keep working.
  • 🧹 Reclaim disk space: Settings → Workspace Cleanup can delete Orca, Claude Code and Codex chats from deleted workspaces. It is off by default; enabling it asks for confirmation and includes previously deleted workspaces. Deleted chats cannot be recovered.
  • ⌨️ Return to the same terminal folder: Panes remember where you last changed directory across restarts, including in folder workspaces. Windows also fixes stray characters after returning to a busy pane.
  • 🔍 See who changed a line: The Git blame status item shows the cursor line’s author and date. In repo workspaces, click it to open the commit; enable Inline git blame in Settings → Editor for annotations beside your code.
  • 👤 Make account switching clearer: Name saved Claude and Codex accounts and see their last observed plans. When you accept Restart or use Always restart, busy Claude terminal sessions resume on the selected account without waiting for their current work to finish.

Highlights

  • Set up an agent in one card: Choose the agent, model and supported effort or Fast options together, then use Open as chat or Open as terminal. Where offered, Default effort lets the agent choose its own level.
  • Control interruptions: Right-click a workspace to mute its notifications while keeping its unread dot. Sleeping workspaces stay asleep after relaunch, and the workspace-card preview lets you try different styles.
  • Use your language and plugins: Discover community translations in Settings or onboarding. Plugin update reviews name the added access and can group approvals; eligible updates can be approved from their notification.
  • Save remote files for later: Choose Download… on a remote file link to save it without opening another app. Windows also recognizes project folders on drive letters mapped to WSL distros.
  • Resolve a blocked Codex action: Chat explains an automatic-review denial and offers Approve once for that action.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Codex history sharing: With sharing enabled, accounts move to one conversation store before their next launch; this may delay the first launch, and busy accounts migrate later. Turning sharing off leaves shared history in place; long conversations started while sharing was on need it enabled to resume. After a Windows downgrade, additional accounts fall back to your default Codex sign-in until you update again.
  • Windows terminals: Close and reopen existing terminals to pick up the stray-character fix.
  • Repo setup steps: Earlier orca.yaml approvals now count as trust for future versions of that repo’s setup commands. VM creation and repos sharing an ID across hosts keep per-version approval.

volcengine/OpenViking ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
react@0.1.14@openviking/react 0.1.14 Stable2026-11-18T09:45:18Z

en-US

Minor Changes

  • 128b63d: AgentConnect accepts isEnterprise to show English or Chinese team onboarding instructions for agents supported by the official installer, preserving API key masking and copying. Existing instructions remain the default.

  • 196fad5: Add OpenViking CLI session, file download, and OVPack commands to Console. Local inputs use the browser file picker. Administrator-only backup and restore require opting in through commandOptions.allowAdminCommands.

    With --read-content, Console search results display multiline file content and fall back to the abstract when content is unavailable or blank. Search result items support an optional content field for the multiline body.

  • 81c42ac: Align the Console's built-in command options with the Volcengine terminal, including filesystem limits, search filters, ACL JSON, inline writes, resource import options, and task filters. Show the canonical names, aliases, and defaults in command help. tree now requires a directory URI, for example ov tree viking://resources -L 2.

  • d510934: FileBrowser can show test materials, group source trajectories by update, and display trajectory documents and source messages when the host supplies the corresponding loaders.

  • a8d37f3: Show timestamped transcripts and extracted frames in FileBrowser for processed video segments. Select a transcript entry to seek the video, or open an extracted frame at full size.

  • b90eb2e: Show Create Wiki and Scheduled Report shortcuts in managed VikingBot. Use the toolbar @ or type @ in the draft to browse and search for files or directories to include with a message.

    After switching languages, select another shortcut to replace the previous preset draft.

Patch Changes

  • 0019eb7: Keep the active video transcript entry highlighted and in view during playback. Show extracted frame filenames, keep thumbnails stable when resizing the preview, and expand the video and transcript together while preserving playback.

  • b62b243: The command console accepts Han characters in compile configuration names, up to 32 characters in total. ASCII-only names allow up to 64 characters.

  • a961625: Allow Console hosts to control selected directory references while keeping the built-in picker and search. Reset the selection when switching or starting a conversation. Chat adapters can acknowledge accepted input before the reply arrives so a submitted clarification card can close promptly.

  • d510934: FileBrowser shows the appropriate empty state when a directory has no L0 or L1 summary.

  • 1c25783: Align the version history and copy icons in the FileBrowser editor with the console. Hide the dashboard tab during editing, show a folder-specific empty message, and label the shared resources root.

  • 84e5f15: Keep retrieval mode selection beside the FileBrowser search field, including when there are no matches. Switching modes retains the query and compatible filters.

  • 3e229d1: Prevent the copy-path tooltip from opening after a mouse-driven rollback when focus moves to the copy button. The tooltip still opens on hover or keyboard focus.

  • b90eb2e: Make selected text stand out in the file editor, including on Wiki conflict lines.

  • 85b3666: Hide in-progress resource import notifications in Console. Import progress stays visible in the command result, and Console still shows completion and failure notifications.

  • 85b3666: Reveal the first conflict when opening the Wiki editor.

  • 85b3666: Align the VikingBot reference picker with the file tree's root order.

  • 85b3666: Close a submitted clarification when VikingBot resumes.

  • b90eb2e: Enable the Load more and Retry buttons in the VikingBot reference picker so users can continue browsing directory pages and recover from listing errors.

  • 13282ba: Show Rename and Delete in a per-session menu in VikingBot history.

  • 85b3666: Keep the VikingBot history popover at a stable height during refresh, and show a centered diamond loader when switching conversations. Add session rename and delete actions when the session service supports them.

    Dismiss pending rename and delete confirmations when the session account or service changes. Standalone VikingBot hosts can provide sessions.scopeKey to identify their session scope.

  • b90eb2e: Show a progress notification while FileBrowser saves Wiki changes, including conflict resolutions.

  • 1c25783: Highlight unresolved Wiki conflicts in the FileBrowser editor and show their explanations on hover. Conflict metadata stays out of the draft.

  • 6e8578d: Show the original Wiki conflict trailer in green at the end of the editor. The trailer is read-only, and saving still submits the editable body.

  • a86d3a4: Align Markdown and YAML syntax colors and the Wiki conflict tooltip with the console editor. Load YAML front matter highlighting regardless of when the Markdown grammar is registered.

  • b90eb2e: Label versions created by a Wiki rollback and show a confirmation after restoring a version or selecting identical content.

zh-CN

功能更新

  • 128b63d: AgentConnect 新增 isEnterprise，为支持官方安装器的 Agent 展示中英文团队接入文案，复用 API Key 脱敏和复制逻辑。默认继续使用原有文案。

  • 196fad5: Console 新增 OpenViking CLI 会话、文件下载和 OVPack 命令。本地输入通过浏览器文件选择器提供。管理员备份和恢复需要通过 commandOptions.allowAdminCommands 显式启用。

    Console 检索命令使用 --read-content 时展示多行正文，正文不可读或为空白时回退到摘要。检索结果项支持通过可选的 content 字段提供多行正文。

  • 81c42ac: 对齐 Console 内置命令与火山引擎终端的参数，包括文件系统限制、检索过滤、ACL JSON、正文写入、资源导入和任务筛选。在命令帮助中展示规范参数名、别名和默认值。tree 现在需要传入目录 URI，例如 ov tree viking://resources -L 2。

  • d510934: 宿主提供相应的数据源后，文件浏览区块可分页展示测试素材，按经验更新分组展示来源轨迹，并在详情中查看轨迹正文和来源消息。

  • a8d37f3: FileBrowser 支持展示已解析视频切片的带时间戳转录文稿和抽帧图片。点击文稿可跳转到对应播放位置，点击抽帧可放大图片。

  • b90eb2e: 内置 VikingBot 新增「创建 Wiki」和「定时日报」快捷入口；可点击工具栏的 @ 或在输入框键入 @ 浏览和搜索文件或目录，并随消息发送引用。

    切换语言后，点击快捷入口仍可替换之前填入的预设草稿。

修复与改进

  • 0019eb7: 播放视频时高亮当前转录文稿并自动滚动。显示抽帧文件名，拖动预览分栏时保持缩略图尺寸稳定，并支持在弹窗中同步查看视频和文稿，保留播放进度。

  • b62b243: 命令台支持使用汉字创建和运行编译配置，含汉字的名称总长最多 32 个字符，纯 ASCII 名称最多 64 个字符。

  • a961625: 允许宿主控制 Console 的目录引用选中项，同时保留内置目录树和搜索。切换或新建会话时清空引用。对话适配器可以在回复到达前确认输入已送达，让已提交的澄清卡及时关闭。

  • d510934: 文件浏览区块对未生成摘要的目录显示对应的 L0 和 L1 空态。

  • 1c25783: 对齐文件浏览区块编辑态的版本历史和复制图标，编辑时隐藏看板页签。展开空文件夹时显示专用提示，并为顶层资源目录添加共享标签。

  • 84e5f15: 文件浏览器在搜索框旁常驻检索方式选择器，无匹配结果时也可以切换。切换时保留输入词和新模式支持的筛选。

  • 3e229d1: 修复鼠标确认回滚后，焦点落到复制路径按钮时误弹提示的问题。悬停和键盘聚焦仍会显示提示。

  • b90eb2e: 调整文件编辑器的文字选区颜色，使选区在 Wiki 冲突行上也清楚可见。

  • 85b3666: 隐藏 Console 中进行中的资源导入通知。命令结果仍显示导入进度，Console 仍会显示任务完成和失败通知。

  • 85b3666: 打开 Wiki 编辑器时定位首个冲突。

  • 85b3666: 统一 VikingBot 引用选择器与文件树的根目录顺序。

  • 85b3666: VikingBot 继续运行后关闭已提交的澄清弹窗。

  • b90eb2e: 修复 VikingBot 引用选择器的「加载更多」和「重试」按钮，支持继续浏览目录分页并在目录加载失败后重试。

  • 13282ba: VikingBot 历史会话将重命名和删除收拢到每行的更多操作菜单。

  • 85b3666: 刷新时保持 VikingBot 历史会话弹层高度稳定，切换会话时居中显示菱形加载动画。会话服务支持相应操作时，提供会话改名和删除入口。

    切换会话账号或服务时关闭已打开的重命名和删除确认框。独立使用 VikingBot 时，宿主可通过 sessions.scopeKey 标识会话作用域。

  • b90eb2e: FileBrowser 保存 Wiki 改动时显示进度通知，包括冲突解决后的保存。

  • 1c25783: 在文件浏览区块的编辑器中高亮未解决的 Wiki 冲突，悬停时显示冲突说明。冲突元数据仍与草稿正文分离。

  • 6e8578d: 在 Wiki 编辑器末尾以绿色显示原始冲突尾注。尾注只读，保存继续提交可编辑正文。

  • a86d3a4: 将 Markdown、YAML 语法配色及 Wiki 冲突气泡与控制台编辑器对齐。Markdown 语法提前注册时也能高亮 YAML 文件头。

  • b90eb2e: 在版本历史中标记 Wiki 回滚生成的版本，并提示回滚完成或所选内容与当前文件一致。

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.35CC Switch v4.0.35 Stable2026-11-18T07:46:34Z

CC Switch v4.0.35

本版本让 Claude Code / Claude Desktop 里用 ChatGPT 账号的 Codex 卡片能用上 GPT-6.2，会话管理里可以看到 Codex /goal 的完整目标，请求详情里的请求 ID 可以一键复制。

另外修复了 Claude Code 的 auto 模式在关不掉思考的模型上被整体拦下；Codex 聚合模式和「打开终端」报 Responses-Lite 需要 reasoning.context；Codex 压缩上下文后第一条请求被拒；用量页自定义日期选到很早的年份时卡死；Skill 的托管副本不在了就卸载不掉等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

会话管理里显示 Codex /goal 的完整目标

目标超过 700 字节时，Codex 注入的上下文里不写目标内容，会话管理里也就看不到目标（见 #9647）。现在从会话记录里读出完整目标，显示成一步「目标 · 状态」，展开即是完整内容；目标和状态都没变的重复记录不再显示。

请求详情里可以复制完整的请求 ID

请求详情里过长的值会被省略号截断，像 Codex 的 resp_... 这类长请求 ID 没法完整复制去对日志（见 #9681）。现在请求 ID 后面多了一个复制按钮。

新增 GPT-6.2 Sol / Astra 的价格

按官方标准档（272K 以内）的价格计费；长上下文档、Batch / Fast 等档位和 GPT-6 一样暂不区分。

修复

  • Claude Code / Claude Desktop 里用 ChatGPT 账号的 Codex 卡片获取不到 GPT-6.2，手动填了也报「not supported when using Codex with a ChatGPT account」（#9678，见 #9670）：OpenAI 按 Codex 客户端版本决定给哪些模型，CC Switch 报的版本太旧。现在按 Codex 0.169.0 请求，没有写死任何模型 ID。
  • Claude Code 的 auto 模式在关不掉思考的模型上，Bash、MCP 等工具调用全部被拒（见 #8887、#9671）：auto 模式的判断请求要求关闭思考，MiniMax-M3.1-Flash 这类模型直接报 400，Claude Code 就认为判断服务不可用。现在经路由时（Claude Code 和 Claude Desktop），Anthropic 格式的上游这样报错会去掉关闭思考的字段，在同一家重试一次。上游本来接受的请求原样发送，不影响缓存。
  • Codex 聚合模式下报「Responses-Lite requires reasoning.context to be all_turns」，同一家直连和路由都正常（见 #9665）：卡片没写模型目录时，聚合模式改用和直连一样的 GPT-6 官方设置。从卡片「打开终端」时出现的同一个报错也一并修好。
  • Codex 经路由转成 Chat 格式时，压缩上下文后第一条请求被要求严格的网关以「message content cannot be empty」拒绝（见 #9660）：压缩时只含图片的用户消息会变成空消息，现在转成 Chat 格式时去掉内容为空的用户消息，其他消息不变。
  • 用量页自定义日期的起点选到 1905 年这类很早的年份时，客户端卡死（见 #9691）：现在只能选从第一条用量记录到今天之间的日期，范围外的日期不可选，手动输入的也会被拉回范围内。
  • Skill 的托管副本已经不在时卸载不掉（#9669，见 #9668）。

致谢

感谢 @imfuxiao（Codex 卡片支持 GPT-6.2）、@Maharramoff（卸载托管副本已不在的 Skill）、@SaladDay（拆分本地路由的转发代码、数据库访问改用应用类型）的贡献。

特别感谢以下贡献者：你们的 PR 发现并修复了真实的问题，只是同样的问题后来在主线上另行修好，PR 因此关闭。@xunapro（#4262，同类 PR 里最早提出 S3 服务商选择没被记住、阿里云 OSS / 腾讯云 COS 因路径式地址报 403）、@mercutiojohn（#8547，找准了 Gemini CLI 供应商获取模型列表失败的三个原因）。

感谢 @chencyan364、@DemosHume、@LAN-SHH、@mouse8867、@leafsnow、@zhuqiang1248、@ramonmi、@zx2121651、@VICCC-0x90 提出需求、报告问题和提供复现信息。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

View release →

debpalash/VoiceStudio ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
v0.5.9-beta.6v0.5.9-beta.6 — VoiceStudio Prerelease2026-11-18T05:21:42Z

Download

Platform Download Signing
Windows x64 Installer ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Windows ARM64 (experimental) Installer ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
macOS Apple Silicon DMG ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
macOS Intel DMG ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
Linux x64 AppImage · deb — Linux packages are not code-signed
Android APK · AAB ✅ Signed APK (signature verified); AAB is the Play upload bundle
iPhone Unsigned IPA ⚠️ Unsigned: sign it with your own Apple ID to sideload; not a TestFlight build

Highlights

  • GTX 900/1000-series NVIDIA cards run on the GPU again with a CUDA 12.6 PyTorch build (#4242)
  • Close to tray: keep VoiceStudio and its jobs running when you close the window (#4174)
  • Download buttons work in the Docker/web build (#4224)
  • Faster-feeling starts: no more "first run" hint on every launch, and slow cold loads are no longer killed after 300 s (#4228)
  • Security hardening across file actions, HTTP errors and CI, plus clearer Windows setup errors (#4173–#4188, #4213, #4230)

Changed

  • Opt-in automatic failure reports now name which part of the app failed (main process, GPU or helper process, GPU job timeout or network) instead of reporting an unknown error (#4243)
  • Removed the unused retired desktop release cleanup helper and its ownership entry. (#4172) — thanks @salemalkadadi-debug!
  • Removed an unused legacy update-busy helper while retaining active restart protection (#4149) — thanks @themrbeasley!
  • Retired an unused legacy update toast while preserving the active Electron update controls (#4141) — thanks @themrbeasley!
  • Retired the unreachable legacy shared UI and the guard tests that held it, repointing the live-behaviour guards to the renderer, and dropped 12 unused npm packages (#4095)
  • Smaller install: removed unused leftover images and old helper code from the app source (#4087)
  • Projects grid view stays smooth with thousands of items by drawing only the cards in view (#4079)
  • Long Projects, dub project and Transcribe history lists now render only the rows on screen, so they stay fast with thousands of items (#4074)
  • Idle CPU: a hidden or minimised window no longer runs polling timers or elapsed-time clocks, and they refresh once when it is shown again (#4073)
  • Removed unused renderer files and 477 unused translation strings, and locale:check now fails on an English key no code uses (#4047)
  • Removed more unused legacy shared components, utilities and API helpers from the old shared UI folder (#4045)
  • Local-model folder saves no longer unload the engine when the stored folder did not change (#4043)
  • Removed more unused legacy settings, gallery and engine screens from the old shared UI folder (#4042)
  • Idle polling is relaxed: the backend health probe slows when steady, cloud-sync polling pauses while the window is hidden, and the Windows parent watcher polls less often (#4041)
  • Removed more unused legacy dubbing screens and hooks from the old shared UI folder (#4040)
  • Release notes now open with one Download table listing every desktop and phone download with its signing, followed by the Highlights (#4037)
  • Removed about 25,000 lines of unused legacy interface code, two unused font packages and the always-skipped weekly evals job; the app itself is unchanged (#2603, #4032)

Added

  • Windows and Linux x64 PCs whose NVIDIA cards are all GTX 900/1000-series (compute capability 5.0 to 6.x, driver 525 or newer) now run on the GPU with a CUDA 12.6 PyTorch build, a one-time ~3 GB download on the next launch; voices, projects and models are kept (#4242)
  • Settings → General can keep VoiceStudio and its running jobs alive in the tray when you close the window, instead of quitting (#4174)
  • Stories can merge adjacent lines with the same speaker and compatible voice/speed settings while preserving text and inline directions — thanks cvak on Discord! (#4161)
  • Added separately opted-in failure reporting through Supermetry app intake across desktop, mobile and server installs, with private metadata and consent-controlled retries (#4137)
  • Dubbing exports the selected transcript as plain UTF-8 TXT, including edited text before generating a dub, with native Save As and paired phone sharing. (#4155) — thanks @shivamdhiman999111-source!
  • A dub interrupted by a crash or restart now picks up from its finished segments instead of starting over (#4088)
  • omnivoice-gguf now supports voice design, non-verbal tags and the decode-steps slider (#4084) — thanks @snazzybean!
  • Cebuano is now a dub translation target (#4058, #4072) — thanks @onparr-24!
  • Home shows a What's new link, winter pixel snow, a Christmas-week Santa, two rows of contributor portraits, and an easter egg that hides the credits until the next update after you visit three contributor profiles (#4070)
  • Voice Cloning has a generation queue: takes started while one is rendering wait their turn and run one after another — thanks @ItzSudhan! (#4064)
  • Audiobooks and Stories have a New project button that starts an empty draft without touching saved projects (#4055)

Docs

  • The localization contract no longer exempts the removed legacy shared i18n catalog, which pointed reviewers at a path that does not exist (#4186) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Document the enforced up-to-date merge gate while the repository cannot use GitHub merge queues (#4165)
  • Track the unresolved VoxCPM2 installation report separately from GPU fallback fixes, reported by vinhson (#4154)
  • Track the unresolved Windows model-loading crash and distinguish merged installer repairs from released fixes (#4146) — thanks @renaldyramadani123-code!
  • Installation tracking separates shipped fixes, pending repairs and unresolved report evidence (#4121)
  • Document host-managed Tailscale access for Docker without sharing the host daemon socket (#4111) — thanks @MaverickKB!

Fixed

  • Windows setup now explains a torch c10.dll "WinError 1114" load failure, naming an outdated Visual C++ runtime when that is the cause and listing what to try otherwise (#4230)
  • Trimmed speech now ends with a short fade-out instead of a sharp cut-off (#4229)
  • The startup screen only says "first run can take several minutes" while the Python environment is actually being installed, and the OmniVoice engine now reports progress during cold imports, reference transcription and synthesis so slow starts are no longer killed as "sent nothing for 300s" (#4228)
  • Windows ROCm setup prefers the AMD driver's newer HIP runtime, fixing an amdhip64_7.dll crash on dGPU + Ryzen iGPU systems, with automatic fallback to the bundled runtime (#4225) — thanks @giuseppefaro!
  • Download buttons for generated audio, batch and audiobook exports now work in the Docker/web build (#4222, #4224) — thanks @msy-jc!
  • Windows startup errors distinguish Smart App Control evaluation mode from an active policy block (#4213) — thanks @isnaenihidayat!
  • Safari extension errors with WebKit top-level stack frames no longer open the repair and bug-report prompt (#4211) — thanks @karlnorling!
  • Dubbing keeps API requests responsive while marking fresh speech on the watermark worker; recovered speech retains its existing mark (#4198) — thanks @swax!
  • Stories and Audiobook keep saved audio with its original connection and offer an explicit availability check for older results (#4196)
  • The changelog reference allowlist no longer outlives the entries it exempts: two dead exemptions were removed and a guard now reports any future ones (#4188) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • HTTP error messages no longer interpolate raw exception text: filesystem and parsing failures return fixed, actionable text, and the remaining details pass through core.scrub.scrub_text to redact secrets and home paths (#4183) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Release preparation no longer aborts when the contributors block cannot be assembled, and a missing previous release only skips that block (#4181) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • The commit identity check now resolves to a definite status when a pull request moves mid-run, instead of leaving a required check pending forever (#4180) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • The gallery pin check now validates a pull request with the checker from its base commit, so a PR cannot move the pin and weaken the check in one commit (#4179) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Bundled installs that cannot run database migrations now enforce one cloud-sync identity per voice, so a duplicate identity is caught locally instead of surfacing later as a sync conflict (#4176) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Phone model catalogues generate on Windows again: the hash-pinned and byte-compared contract artifacts keep LF endings in every checkout (#4175) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Restrict native file-opening actions to the primary app window while preserving trusted recorder exports and window-local controls (#4173) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Community Gallery limits mounted cards in large catalogues while retaining the current preview, keyboard focus and paging (#4169)
  • Desktop failure observers retain the original consent during backend restarts and preserve normal Promise behavior. (#4209)
  • CosyVoice catalogue downloads skip unused alternative model files and use the same required assets as managed installs. (#4207)
  • Android keeps large manuscripts editable and recoverable, and retains reporting consent through permission prompts. (#4206) — thanks @gaino-svg!
  • The closed-PR CI sweep requests cancellations incrementally, preserves partial progress and reports incomplete searches or skipped requests accurately (#4189) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Kept automatic error reports bound to original consent through delayed work and recovery, and preserved native CI verification (#4184) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Windows ROCm setup preserves working-install stamps, verifies the pinned interpreter, and reports native crash exit codes (#4182) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Windows checkouts no longer fail the CLI packaging tests: the Debian maintainer-script cases skip when the host has no POSIX shell or symlink privileges (#4178) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Phone dubbing clip/stem exports check output space on both platforms and avoid false low-space errors on iOS (#4168)
  • Linux ROCm setup skips CUDA downloads and preserves its installed PyTorch stack on resume (#4167) — thanks @khryztiam!
  • GPU readiness and in-process routing reflect completed self-test failures (#4166) — thanks @khryztiam!
  • CLA audits keep exempt accounts visible for provenance review without reporting a missing required signature (#4164)
  • Release rehearsals show measured Android APK signing, and tagged downloads require matching signature evidence (#4163)
  • Manual Windows crash reports retain the faulting thread ahead of verbose status tails — thanks @renaldyramadani123-code and @freemood36-svg! (#4162)
  • macOS publishing checks Gatekeeper even when Windows installers are explicitly unsigned (#4153)
  • The reviewed model-install steps now require the same authenticated consumer as the rest of the model manager (#4152) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Diagnostic bundles and Electron bug reports redact share PINs and encoded credential query parameters (#4151) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Make the installed-model licence action open its terms and acknowledgement dialog — thanks hugotriunfa18 on Discord! (#4147)
  • Android model downloads resume through VPNs while preserving Wi-Fi policy and saved ranges (#4140) — thanks @JerryTimi!
  • Stopped playback errors from incorrectly claiming that generated audio files are missing (#4138) — thanks @AAYUSH-CODER1904!
  • Transcriptions can save clean plain text and timed SRT captions, with an explanation when subtitle timings are unavailable (#4135)
  • Cloud sign-in now rejects a discovery document whose token or revocation endpoint is not on the issuer's own origin (#4133) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Segmented model downloads pin public destinations with bounded DNS checks across redirects, ranges and proxies, preventing private-network fallback and CDN credential leaks (#4132) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • The LM Studio model probe no longer sends the stored API key in clear text to a remote host unless plaintext LAN is allowed (#4131) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • A deterministic test now fails when a new mutating API route ships without an authorization decision (#4130) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Docker deployments now require the administrator API key for the runtime log, log stream, recent errors and crash report, as the install docs already said (#4129) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • MCP tools preserve remote caller permissions across local and HTTP backends, including failed transport lookups (#4128) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • TTS and event WebSockets now enforce the same loopback/credential rule as dictation, closing a share-PIN bypass (#4126) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Imported project bundles can no longer make ffmpeg fetch remote URLs through a playlist-shaped media file (#4125) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Deleting history, rewriting dub scripts and clearing model-licence receipts now require an administrator, as the API docs already promised (#4124) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • SoniTranslate installation and start/stop now require a local administrator, like every other engine installer (#4123) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Model deletion over the API now requires administrator rights (#4122) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Crash recurrence reports recognize current Electron version fields and distinguish beta releases (#4120)
  • Reclaim idle GPU models before generation when VRAM or RAM is low (#4117) — thanks @vandyahmad24!
  • Model downloads reject damaged files and retry without reusing corrupt ranges, while cancellation preserves verified work (#4116)
  • Reuse verified setup wheels on low-space retries and handle interrupted size probes without an uncaught error. (#4115)
  • Saved and inline pronunciation choices apply consistently across speech, dubbing, batch and voice-conversion routes (#4114) — thanks @nntloc!
  • Stop cancelled setup retries promptly and keep verbose runtime probes from blocking on their diagnostic output. (#4113)
  • Long recordings use duration-scaled transcription limits across uploads, dubbing, child engines and MCP tools (#4112) — thanks @xabiherdz-svg!
  • URL imports restore their connection guard after temporary DNS resolver changes (#4110)
  • Update the optional Dagger pipeline's OpenTelemetry dependencies to fix excessive CPU use when parsing malformed trace context. (#4109)
  • Optional analytics no longer delays backend readiness, and consent changes invalidate queued startup work (#4108) — thanks @cf3901646!
  • Auto setup can retry PyPI failures through the regional mirror and report the actual failed host (#4107) — thanks @mrkeshi!
  • Malformed model-install status responses keep the interface open and preserve the last valid download state (#4106) — thanks @ftlcustomerservice73-ui!
  • Windows installer process checks skip custom PowerShell profiles, and source installs use shorter build paths to avoid MSVC linker failures (#4099)
  • Restarting to install an update keeps update status available while the local engine shuts down, avoiding missing-handler errors (#4098)
  • Automatic blank-renderer recovery keeps the open workspace instead of returning Stories or Audiobook to Home (#4097)
  • Dubbing keeps the saved recording for cloned and designed voices with style instructions, so each segment uses the selected speaker's reference (#4093)
  • Setup on Auto-detect now retries a stalled Python download (cpython-… (download)) once from the other source, GitHub or its mirror, instead of stalling again (#4085)
  • A refused cross-site API request now names its origin and gives the exact OMNIVOICE_ALLOWED_ORIGINS line to add, so other local apps such as AIRI can be connected (#4083)
  • Setup no longer crashes with "Cannot set property code" when a runtime download or check is cancelled on Windows (#4082)
  • MOSS-TTS-Nano speaks again on a fresh one-click install: the transformers 5 shim no longer breaks model loads in its pinned transformers 4 environment (#4063, #4078) — thanks @RerankerGuo, and @rockziepromax for the report!
  • OmniVoice GGUF falls back to the CPU when the GPU backend crashes, for example Vulkan on a GTX 1080 (#4050, #4072) — thanks @MeinLive!
  • Full dubs no longer fail when translation fills segments with free-text directions like "slow"; unsupported ones are dropped for that segment (#4054, #4072) — thanks @user555gtb!
  • Audiobook MP3 exports play and download again instead of returning 404 (#4071, #4072) — thanks @dimfk001git!
  • Colab: setup cells show the failing command's output, the security-floor check judges the Mako copy Python imports, and the web UI loads through the new *.prod.r.colab.dev proxy — thanks @hkarmoush! (#4069)
  • Turning on LAN sharing no longer crashes the backend with an Internal Server Error — thanks @blutuxyz! (#4059)
  • The OmniVoice GGUF engine finds its bundled runtime again in installed apps instead of reporting it missing — thanks @b84330808! (#4053)
  • When the operating system times out starting the engine (spawn ETIMEDOUT, seen on macOS), VoiceStudio retries twice and then says to restart the computer (#4039)
  • Settings → Updates lists only your channel's releases, and Beta suggests switching to Stable when a newer stable is out (#4038)
  • SSML [spell] keeps flag, ZWJ-joined and skin-tone emoji whole instead of splitting them (#4020) — thanks @kevin9327!

CI

  • CI actions gain artifact and no-push Docker contract checks, with one bounded mobile artifact-upload retry (#4192)
  • CI pins the version of the security analyser it installs at run time, so a new release cannot silently change the findings (#4187) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Documentation and inventory changes run a read-only docs drift check on relevant pull requests, with rolling-issue maintenance kept in nightly/manual jobs (#4185) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Container builds grant the registry-write token only to the jobs that publish images, not to every job in the workflow (#4177) — thanks @DeryFerd!

  • Windows release and optional-engine tests use portable text fixtures, native Bash and normalized cache paths (#4197) — thanks @swax!
  • Release builds load app-intake credentials from GitHub Actions Secrets without public-variable fallbacks (#4204, #4205)
  • Fixed Android screenshot capture waiting behind the optional error-report prompt (#4202) — thanks @velixio!
  • Keep iOS voice-search and download checks reliable across saved model choices (#4171)
  • The iPhone smoke test waits for typed text to reach the editor instead of reading it once, removing a CI flake (#4100)
  • MOSS compatibility tests keep checking the active Transformers version after another test restores imported modules (#4096)
  • The iPhone UI smoke test keeps its slow-inventory case loading for the whole test, so a slow simulator no longer fails it (#4076)
  • Docker's web build no longer fails the unused-key check when only electron/ is copied, and the iPhone CI job allows for a native-runtime cache miss (#4052)
  • Removed unused scripts, the old Tauri MSI tooling and leftover Turborepo ignore lines (#4046)
  • Accessibility smoke timeouts now name the requests still pending, so a flaky CI failure points at its cause (#4036)

openclaw/openclaw ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2026.10.33openclaw 2026.10.33 Stable2026-11-18T04:15:53Z

This is a gateway-only extended-stable release, which is our current equivalent to LTS. This release is OpenClaw from the end of October 2026, plus critical security updates, reliability and performance fixes, and features like new model support. The latest version of OpenClaw at the time of this release is 2026.11.5

2026.10.33

This extended-stable release brings cumulative Gateway and core-plugin improvements since 2026.9.37, plus the selected maintenance fixes on the approved 2026.11.4 product baseline. Distribution covers the core npm package, matching official npm plugins, and Gateway Docker images; native-app releases are not included.

Highlights

  • Long conversations and large session stores use bounded export and recovery work, reducing out-of-memory failures during chat, memory export, Doctor repair, and Gateway startup. (#194066, #196158, #195459, #196480, #176737, #181289) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa, @vincentkoc, @ericcaiwx-star, @obviyus, @garrytan, and @oxen-horse.
  • Interrupted updates recover when their executor dies instead of leaving the Gateway stuck offline, while version-skew checks protect state still used by an installed service. (#181695, #186971, #187819) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa, @PonySlayer28, @tomdailey55, @jalehman, @fuller-stack-dev, and @vincentkoc.
  • Replies survive more restart and reconnect paths, preserving original prompts, recovering Telegram responses, and avoiding lost WhatsApp replies or skipped Signal messages. (#187157, #195400, #168778, #187667) Thanks @shakkernerd, @jason-allen-oneal, @mcaxtr, @HermanZeng, and @obviyus.
  • Shared-session privacy is stronger: Side chat stays private to its viewer, internal model prompts stay out of displayed output, and compaction redacts credentials in quoted summaries. (#185390, #173460, #197349) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @Satoshi-Kusumoto, @RomneyDa, @zhangguiping-xydt, @Patrick-Erichsen, and @obviyus.
  • Automations can react to polled conditions and MCP events, run repeatable scripts, and use explicitly allowed MCP servers. (#101195, #165399, #160996, #160995) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @obviyus.
  • Installed skills can be searched and read on demand, helping agents find relevant instructions without putting every installed skill into the prompt. (#158089, #158090, #158091, #160538) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa and @vincentkoc.
  • Sessions can import existing Claude Code or Codex transcripts and move between local and cloud environments, with repository recipes and clearer cloud-startup progress. (#161955, #171690, #170862, #170895) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @joshavant and @RomneyDa.

Additional maintenance fixes

  • Provider continuity: preserve server compaction checkpoints across credential or model changes on the same provider API and endpoint, avoiding unnecessary history replay. (#200063, #200208) Thanks @obviyus.
  • macOS installation: preserve the controlling terminal during shell installation so onboarding can install plugins without detached Node subprocesses aborting. (#200247) Thanks @miguelbranco80.
  • Update recovery: record successful Gateway startup writes for the next Doctor run without accepting changes that predate startup. (#200260) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Existing device folders: recognize models using the implicit OpenClaw runtime without turning that default into an explicit session override. (#190065) Thanks @obviyus.
  • Plugin compatibility: retain the signed 2026.11.4 source baseline as this release's compatibility identity without changing its displayed version or lowering plugin API requirements. (#200302) Thanks @RomneyDa.

Changes

  • Session organization: snooze conversations, choose a custom wake time, and explicitly organize or archive nested conversations. (#161960, #171255, #168076) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @jacobtomlinson.
  • Session context: show task progress in Details, indicate when a chat is waiting on helpers, and open the Subagents panel from the chat's helper count. (#161674, #164190, #166142) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa, @ceckert, @obviyus, @vyctorbrzezowski, and @shakkernerd.
  • Shared conversations: add emoji reactions to prompts and replies and configure when sessions may send or receive messages. (#161053, #162316) Authored by @steipete.
  • Workspace placement: start sessions in existing folders on paired devices or select hosted workspaces when creating sessions. (#169722, #168259) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @obviyus.
  • First-run setup: automatically choose inference and offer optional memory-embedding setup, including non-interactive embedding-only local setup. (#169628, #163693, #170153) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @PollyBot13 and @dh-js.
  • Memory providers: use a provider-neutral memory runtime with host-resolved audiences and provider-owned pre-compaction flushes. (#162175, #162176, #162177) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa, @vincentkoc, and @jalehman.
  • Context management: use provider-native compaction on supported OpenAI and Anthropic API-key routes, server-side compaction for ChatGPT sign-in sessions, and model fallback after context-overflow recovery. (#168164, #168401, #170141) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @joshavant, @RomneyDa, @ceckert, @obviyus, @goslingmanagment, @dmsrg399, @aniketkrs, and @amirarenco-create.
  • Model setup: support endpoint-resolved custom aliases, show provider recommendations first, and display active authentication subscription blocks. (#170009, #167370, #133087) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa, @ceckert, @obviyus, @xyrolle, @Leon-SK668, and @vincentkoc.

  • Voice calls: add per-call briefs, reports, live steering, callbacks, and voicemail detection; OpenAI realtime voice can use a custom endpoint. (#165716, #165632) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @jacobtomlinson and @mbelinky.
  • Microsoft Teams: support multiple bot accounts. (#112811) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @jimmypuckett.

Wei-Shaw/sub2api ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.2.23Sub2API 0.2.23 Stable2026-11-18T03:51:33Z

AI API Gateway Platform - 将 AI 订阅配额分发和管理

接入 GPT-6.2 Sol 和 Astra，支持 Ultra Fast 档位；修正多条协议转换链路上的缓存写入用量和计费。

新增功能

  • 支持 GPT-6.2 Sol / Astra 模型：价格与官方一致；上下文超过 272K 时按长上下文计费；Fast 档 2 倍价；Ultra Fast 档在 2027-03-01 00:00（太平洋时间）前是 3 倍促销价，之后自动切为 6 倍
  • GPT-6.2 Ultra Fast 按订阅套餐放行：Pro 200 只开放 Sol，Pro 500 开放 Sol 和 Astra
  • 渠道定价新增「Ultra Fast 倍率」，可按渠道覆盖。不填时沿用模型自带倍率，只影响 Ultra Fast 档

优化改进

  • OpenAI 流式请求在首次输出前空闲超时，现在会切换账号重试，不再直接报错给客户端。最多切换 1 次，避免一个卡住的请求把超时惩罚扩散到整个账号池
  • 流式空闲计时改为每收到上游数据就重置，避免持续出数据的长流被误判超时
  • 原生 Chat Completions 流式透传减少 flush 次数，降低网关 CPU 占用
  • DeepSeek reasoning 占位和 Ollama Cloud reasoning 回填改为一次改写请求体，大幅降低长对话请求的 CPU 和内存开销

Bug 修复

  • 修复 Anthropic ↔ OpenAI Responses / Chat Completions 互转时丢失缓存写入 TTL（5m/1h）明细的问题
  • 修复缓存写入明细之和小于总量时少计费的问题，差额现在按 5m 价格补计
  • 修复 OpenAI 网关走 Anthropic 原生路径时 1h 缓存写入按 5m 价格计费的问题，现在按 1h 价格计费
  • 修复流式过程中只更新缓存字段时，prompt 总量和缓存写入总量被覆盖或算错的问题

📥 Installation

Docker:

# Docker Hub
docker pull weishaw/sub2api:0.2.23

# GitHub Container Registry
docker pull ghcr.io/wei-shaw/sub2api:0.2.23

One-line install (Linux):

curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Wei-Shaw/sub2api/main/deploy/install.sh | sudo bash

Manual download: Download the appropriate archive for your platform from the assets below.

📚 Documentation

  • GitHub Repository
  • Installation Guide
