tobi/qmd ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.9.0 Stable2026-11-18T03:48:03Z

[2.9.0] - 2026-11-17

QMD adds document processors, metadata filtering, and remote model support. This release also updates llama.cpp and hardens concurrent indexing and MCP shutdown.

Fixed

  • Serialize trust-record mutations and atomically publish the trust file, so concurrent approvals and revocations do not overwrite each other.

  • Share a crash-recoverable SQLite sidecar lock across CLI, SDK and direct embedding runs, preventing concurrent partial-vector cleanup from deleting another run's completed vectors.

  • Hold the index's embed lock in clearAllEmbeddings, so a clear cannot drop the vector table under a running embed or resurrect vectors it just deleted.

  • Keep SDK configuration sources separate per store. Coordinate LLM sessions, idle unloading and disposal so in-flight operations retain their resources.

  • Wait for admitted SDK operations before closing their store, and reject reentrant closes instead of closing SQLite beneath active work.

  • Bound MCP shutdown to a five-second grace period by default. On timeout, cancel requests and report failure without disposing resources still in use.

  • Stream MCP event responses instead of buffering them indefinitely, and cancel requests when their clients disconnect.

  • Serialize overlapping scans on one connection and make processor publication and embedding retry states explicit.

  • Reconcile document changes made during CJK FTS shadow rebuilding before publishing, so concurrent writes remain searchable.

  • Serialize YAML configuration mutations and publish them atomically, retaining concurrent edits and the permissions and targets of existing config files. Synchronize SQLite against the current locked config snapshot so an older opener cannot remove another process's newly added collection, and publish SDK collection and context changes before mirroring them, so a refused config write leaves no database-only edit or stale config_hash. A rename records the old→new pair before publishing, so an interrupted index move is finished by repeating the command and is never inferred from a name that merely exists; repeating a removal finishes it the same way. While that marker is live, a second rename or any change to either name is refused with PendingRenameError instead of destroying the only evidence, and an unreadable or malformed marker fails closed with CorruptPendingRenameError rather than being read as "no rename".

  • Coordinate MCP daemon PID-file claims and stale recovery between current versions, preventing concurrent starters from displacing a live daemon.

  • Avoid leaking Nix libc to host subprocesses, and use the build target instead of running uname to select the Darwin launcher mitigation.

  • Expose the matching C++ runtime in the Linux Nix development shell so Bun can load the packaged llama.cpp backend.

  • Correct the Python and Go grammar repair hints to use their pinned versions.

  • Keep memory-regression coverage effective when Bun's SQLite does not track allocations for hard_heap_limit.

  • Security: MCP stdio and HTTP startup now enforce project-local config trust before selecting models, ignoring all unapproved model URIs and options, including remote embedding credential environment variable names.

  • qmd doctor selects vector sample identities before loading document bodies, avoiding excessive SQLite memory use on large indexes with duplicate paths. #978 (thanks @naveenspark)

  • Vector diagnostics match passages by their saved character position, avoiding false mismatches when earlier chunks change the sequence numbering. #978 (thanks @naveenspark)

Added

  • Check bounded TLA+ models for lock, trust, indexing, processor, SDK and server lifecycles in CI, including intentional counterexample controls.

  • Per-collection processors turn source files into stable-key Markdown index documents through explicit SDK callbacks or trusted argv commands. Bounded whole-source validation and SQL savepoints retain the last complete result on processing failures; source fingerprints skip unchanged work and preserve unchanged output vectors. Empty coverage and processor-mode markers prevent silent raw-input fallback after configuration removal. Commands are not an OS sandbox; see docs/PROCESSORS.md for trust, versioning and crash boundaries.

  • Optional TypeSafe System One reranking through models.rerank: typesafe:<model> and copied per-store rerankOptions (revision and credential variable name). Independent per-document scores use bounded two-worker HTTP, caller/session cancellation and revision-specific cache identities, without a local reranker. Public SDK search accepts a signal; duplicate-file chunks cache by result index. Status/doctor do not probe the remote service; pull skips its download. Local models and cache keys are unchanged. Adapted from #1014 (thanks @caseymanos).

  • Explicit OpenAI-compatible remote embeddings through models.embed API-base URIs (http[s]://host/v1#model-id), with bounded requests/responses, caller/session cancellation and opt-in credential environment variable names. SDK and CLI indexing need no local embedding model or tokenizer. Remote space identities pin the endpoint, model, dimensions, revision and input format; vector search enforces remote space boundaries in scope and reads matches from one post-request SQLite snapshot. Remote queries require exact model/ fingerprint matches; local queries refuse remote rows without changing existing local-to-local or legacy-fingerprint behavior. Local GGUF defaults are unchanged. Adapted from #761 (thanks @epruseal).

  • Added Oxlint lint fence.

  • Document metadata and metadata filtering. Markdown documents can opt into typed metadata through a namespaced frontmatter block (qmd.metadata with strings, numbers, booleans, or flat homogeneous arrays), and every search surface — CLI search/vsearch/query via --filter <json>, the SDK's filter option on search()/searchLex()/searchVector(), the MCP query tool, and HTTP POST /query and /search — accepts one shared recursive filter AST discriminated by operator: and/or/not logical groups, eq/ne/gt/gte/lt/lte comparisons, in/nin/all membership, contains/prefix/suffix text matching, type for the stored type of a key's values, and exists presence, with an optional caseInsensitive flag on any condition whose value is a string (ASCII folding). Every returned result satisfies the filter (applied before RRF fusion and reranking); like collection filtering, highly selective filters remain best-effort for top-K completeness. Frontmatter stays ordinary searchable content — no chunking, embedding, snippet, or line-number changes — and documents without qmd.metadata behave exactly as before. JSON/SDK/MCP/HTTP results now include each document's indexed metadata, and qmd status reports how many documents still need metadata extraction (a normal qmd update backfills existing indexes).

  • Metadata discovery. Filtering is only useful when the caller knows what to filter on, so every surface now reports the metadata keys, types, and value counts already in the index — read from the existing metadata tables, with no re-indexing. The CLI adds qmd collection metadata [name...] with --filter <json> to count only documents matching a filter (same AST as search) and --match <json> to select which metadata entries are reported (the same AST evaluated per entry, where a condition's field is the entry's key or value, so one grammar covers a single key, a family of keys, reverse lookup of a value, typed thresholds, and any and/or/not composition), plus --key-limit/--key-offset/--all-keys and --value-limit/--value-offset/--all-values to page both windows, and --sort count|value and --min-count <n> to order and trim values. Every windowed list reports its exact remainder, counts are documents rather than values, numbers report min/median/max, keys whose documents disagree on type are split per type with their own counts, and with --filter every coverage count is measured against the documents the filter admits. qmd collection list names each collection's top keys, qmd collection show details them with a value preview, and qmd status summarizes coverage. The SDK adds listMetadata(options) (the same options, spelled keyLimit, valueLimit, and so on, with MetadataOptionError for a value outside its domain and MetadataBindingBudgetError for a filter and match that together bind too many SQL parameters), the MCP server adds a metadata tool and lists each collection's most covered keys in status, and the HTTP server adds POST /metadata. MetadataFilter and MetadataMatch are both MetadataPredicate<Condition>, one recursive grammar over the conditions a document or a metadata entry admits.

Fixed

  • Filtered vector search binds candidate IDs as one JSON list, so a parser-valid metadata filter cannot exhaust Node's SQL variable limit during document lookup. Applies to both exact scans and the capped global fallback.

  • Collection- and metadata-scoped BM25 search (qmd search -c, the lex leg of qmd query, MCP, SDK searchLex) no longer returns false-empty or incomplete results when stronger matches outside the scope fill the old limit * 10 candidate window (#922). The scope is now applied to the full FTS5 match set, materialized once, so search -c <collection> is exact for common terms; unscoped search keeps its early-terminating plan. A search over several collections, such as the default ones, runs one keyword query instead of one per collection, which made keyword search about 12x faster over 13 collections (thanks @brettdavies). Builds on the approach in #918 (thanks @fxstein). #953 (thanks @Mr-Beasley)

  • qmd update no longer deactivates every document in a collection whose root folder is missing, for example an unmounted drive or an offline network share. The folder globbed to an empty list, which read as "every file was deleted", so search went empty and a qmd cleanup before the drive came back deleted the rows for good. The collection is now reported as not found and its index is left unchanged. Permission and I/O errors still fail with their original cause (#989). #990 (thanks @ParkerRex)

  • Embedding generation and legacy fingerprint adoption now tokenize documents with the store-selected embedding model instead of the global default. This keeps chunk boundaries aligned with the model that creates and verifies the stored vectors without initializing an unrelated provider.

  • qmd doctor no longer stalls or fills the temp directory on large indexes when checking legacy (empty-fingerprint) embeddings. The adoption sample query joined content and grouped by the document body, so SQLite materialized the full body once per legacy chunk and per active path before LIMIT 1 discarded it, the same pattern as the doctor vector-sample check (#978). It now picks the sample row through indexes and loads only that row's body; the sampled chunk is unchanged (#994). #995 (thanks @mjaverto)

Changed

  • Update node-llama-cpp 3.20.0 → 3.22.3 (llama.cpp v0.9.0), MCP server 2.0.0 → 2.4.0, web-tree-sitter 0.26.12 → 0.27.2, and other compatible runtime and development dependencies. Refresh Bun, pnpm and Nix dependency metadata; Vitest stays on 3 and TypeScript on 5 for compatibility.

  • The MCP server caches its instructions per store for up to 60 seconds instead of rebuilding them, with a full index-status scan, for every HTTP request. Concurrent requests share one build and a failed build is not cached; index changes from other processes show up in the instructions within a minute. #815 (thanks @fxstein)

  • Avoid a redundant runtime process on packaged CLI calls where the launcher is already running under its selected Node or Bun runtime. #923 (thanks @ilepn)

  • qmd update no longer reads files whose modification time and size match the last pass (tracked in a new file_sync_state table), so re-indexing an unchanged collection takes seconds. A cached entry counts only while its document is still active at that path with that content, so a collection removed and added back is re-indexed, while a renamed one keeps its entries; files with missing or outdated metadata are still re-extracted. qmd update and qmd collection add skip files over 10 MB with FILE_TOO_LARGE, and qmd update deactivates a previously indexed file that becomes empty or is over 10 MB, including one indexed by an earlier release. #962 (thanks @rikvanriel)

  • Search results carry at most the first 262,144 characters of each document body (qmd search --full, MCP results, keyword and vector hits), which bounds memory on indexes with very large documents. A match past that point is still found, but its snippet, and the passage the reranker scores, come from the start of the document. #962 (thanks @rikvanriel)

  • qmd update and qmd collection add write a collection scan in short transactions instead of committing every row on its own, so a first index of 20,000 files takes about 5 s instead of about 3 minutes. #1021 (thanks @brettdavies)

  • qmd query and qmd vsearch now drop repeated query expansions before searching. The expansion model can repeat a line, and the cache kept every copy: one reported query ran 23 expansions where 9 were distinct, and each copy ran its own search. Cached expansions are deduplicated when read, so existing caches need no rebuild. Repeated lex lines used to count more than once in the rank fusion, so result order can shift slightly. (#921) #1000 (thanks @ParkerRex)

  • Structured searches over several collections (qmd query with lex:/vec:/hyde: lines, the MCP query tool, SDK queries) run one search per line over the whole collection list, a keyword search for a lex: line and a vector search for a vec: or hyde: line, and fuse one ranked list per search. Rankings no longer depend on the order the collections are named. #946 (thanks @shalom-t), #1009 (thanks @xidus90)

Changed

  • The vector index is partitioned by collection. Each embedded chunk is stored once per collection that holds it, under an integer collection id, so a collection-scoped qmd query or qmd vsearch scans only that collection's vectors instead of pre-filtering the whole index, and a small collection is never crowded out of the results by a large one. The first command after upgrading converts the index in place and prints its progress; the conversion resumes from where it stopped if interrupted, two commands started at once share it, and a VACUUM at the end reclaims the space of the old table. qmd collection rename no longer touches vectors, qmd update removes, as it finishes, the vectors of content that no indexed document holds any more (content that comes back later is embedded again), and qmd update and qmd embed copy an already-embedded document into a collection that gains it instead of embedding it again. A metadata-filtered vector search returns the nearest documents the filter admits however many chunks it admits, and a vector search fills its limit even when one long document holds all of the nearest chunks. Downgrading to an older qmd afterwards needs qmd embed -f, and a qmd mcp server started before the upgrade must be restarted. #983 (thanks @brettdavies)

Changed

  • qmd cleanup repacks the vector table when fewer than 90% of its chunk reads hold live rows. vec0 fills the first free slot of its newest chunk and reclaims a chunk only once it is empty, so re-embeds and orphan removal leave holes in older chunks that every brute-force scan still reads; a 794k-vector index at 36% occupancy scanned twice as slowly as a packed copy of the same rows. The repack moves the live rows of each mostly-empty chunk to the tail, one chunk per short transaction, so it never holds the write lock for long and an interrupted run leaves a consistent table. The cleanup output and qmd cleanup --dry-run report the chunk counts. #937 (thanks @brettdavies)

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v16.0.0 Stable2026-11-18T03:11:11Z

My Work puts what needs you first

My Work, the page that shows what your agents are doing, was rebuilt from a full UI review (#6210, #6304). What needs you sits at the top, long lists fold, and every page works on a phone, by keyboard and with a screen reader.

  • What needs you comes first. On the home page the first request waiting on you is at the top, with the one orange button on the screen. Requests are one-line rows; Idle and Shipped fold to one row each, with their counts.
  • Shorter pages. On a real install: home is about 2,600px tall instead of 5,138; the List opens with 5 rows instead of 195; My Rules is about 4,400px instead of over 17,000.
  • Reply where you read. A request's drawer puts the reply box right under its progress line, in every state. While a reply is on its way, "Reply here" waits, so a message can never go to a second conversation as a second paid turn.
  • A focus area's page reads top to bottom: what needs you, the asks not started yet, then the board. A failed save says which change failed and offers Try again on that screen only. Past focus shows the day an area was marked done.
  • My Rules: one rule to decide at a time, with one orange Approve. On a phone, swipe to decide.
  • Fits a phone. Every page is 390px wide on a 390px phone, with a bottom bar for My Work, Focus and My Rules.
  • Keyboard and screen readers. Every control has a name; tooltips open from Tab; focus comes back to where you were after a dialog, the drawer, Dismiss or Try again; controls are 44px on touch.
  • Theme before load. Your light or dark choice is drawn before the first frame, loading and error screens included.
  • Plain errors. A failure is one sentence in your language, with the raw reason folded under Details. Nothing retries by itself.
  • Your language and clock. Times show in your language from the recorded time (French reads a 24-hour clock), and a day from another year shows its year. New strings are translated into French, Korean, Turkish and Chinese; corrections from native speakers are welcome.
  • Less motion. A live update moves at most two things, and nothing moves with reduced motion on.

Observation TV is removed

  • The TV page and its read-only remote access are gone from the open-source worker (#6295): /tv, tv.html and the CLAUDE_MEM_TV_TOKEN setting. The classic viewer and My Work are unchanged.
  • If you watched TV from another machine: set CLAUDE_MEM_LOCAL_API_TOKEN, which protects network access now, and delete CLAUDE_MEM_TV_TOKEN. Until you do, a worker that still finds the TV token with a non-loopback CLAUDE_MEM_WORKER_HOST and no local API token binds to 127.0.0.1 (::1 for an IPv6 host) and logs a SECURITY error, so the full API is never open to the network without a token.
  • Framing protection now covers every page the worker serves.

Already shipped in 15.20.0 and 15.21.0

The rest of the same review reached you in 15.20.0:

  • Setup: a step bar, Back on every screen, plain errors, and Stop import (#6233).
  • Classic viewer: one header row, a searchable project picker, "Find in this session", and no React error 185 at the end of a long timeline (#6229).
  • Import from Mem0 and Supermemory: acceptance fixes (#6206).
  • Fixes: Codex Desktop sessions are read live on macOS (#6285, thanks @MaxWang007), and the worker starts when settings.json is not valid JSON (#6259).

15.21.0 followed: Code Mode starts with claude-mem enabled (#6278), auto-clear resumes with a continuation prompt (#6294), Hermes Desktop and GitHub Copilot in VS Code connect over MCP (#6284, #6286), and setup finishes when your prompt history is too large for one upload (#6280).

Full notes: 15.20.0 and 15.21.0.

Also new

  • The SessionStart banner is a card in Claude Code terminals (#6274). On Claude Code 2.1.326 or later, the Rules + Tools line, "Previously" and the timeline are drawn as one framed card. A session mirrored to the Claude app shows one line instead of the hook's name in front of every line of the banner.

Fixes

  • The worker keeps answering while it works through a backlog. Health checks, hooks and the viewer no longer wait behind:
    • a hook spool backlog: the drain reads files in parallel batches of 64 (#6260, thanks @Zhong-fan) and removes and claims them without blocking (#6296, from #6254 by @Afloat16); reported in #6247 by @soarkit;
    • the project list at worker start, which read every row and now reads the project indexes (#6270; reported in #6246 by @soarkit);
    • the Claude CLI check that corpus prime and query run on a cache miss (#6256, thanks @Afloat16; reported in #6250 by @soarkit);
    • Chroma sync's checkpoint, which was rewritten for every row (about 28 MB of writes for 2,000 rows) and is now written once per 250 ms (#6204, thanks @Zhong-fan; reported in #6203 by @soarkit).
  • Folder CLAUDE.md files: updates fetch up to 8 folders at a time instead of one by one (#6264; reported in #6248 by @soarkit), claude-mem generate reads a project's observations once instead of once per folder (#6265; reported in #6249 by @soarkit), and a failed folder update is logged with its folder and tried again by the next observation for that folder (#6208, thanks @0xABCD01).
  • Long Bash output keeps its end (#6292; reported in #6282 by @micronox). When Claude Code cut an output over about 30,000 characters and saved the full text to a file, the memory came from the start only, so the last lines (test summaries, final errors) were lost. claude-mem now reads the end from that file.
  • Native memory notes: a note with type: at the top of its frontmatter keeps its category (#6244), and a note's project is found when the transcript's first prompt is long (#6272), thanks @Afloat16.
  • Recent sessions include adopted worktrees (#6268, thanks @Afloat16). After a worktree was merged into its parent, OpenClaw's claude-mem-recent showed no previous sessions.
  • Search: Chinese and Japanese terms inside longer unspaced words are found in corpus evidence (#6213, thanks @liuyinchu). smart_outline, smart_search and smart_unfold find JavaScript functions defined by assignment, such as exports.read = function … (#6245), and each Java method overload unfolds on its own (#6257), thanks @Afloat16.
  • Quieter error reports: a context read that passes the 120-second reader deadline names the slow read and logs a warning, not an error (#6255), and on Windows a spool entry that Windows keeps locked gets one warning instead of an error report on every drain (#6269).
  • Two failures that were dropped are logged now (#6302): auto-clear says so in the transcript when it could not hand Claude its request to save the work state, and the server logs why /v1/info could not read the vector search status.
  • Turkish: clearer My Work wording and an up-to-date Turkish README (#6287, thanks @alkank).

For contributors

  • The commit-message test that needs bash 4 is skipped on macOS's bash 3.2, and still runs on Linux CI (#6297).

v15.21.0 Stable2026-11-17T20:54:27Z

claude-mem no longer turns off Claude Code's Code Mode

  • Code Mode starts with claude-mem enabled (#6278, thanks @naive000; reported in #6209 by @nasif-naseef). Code Mode (experimental, Claude Code 2.1.323 and later) refuses to start when a plugin's hooks module hooks tool.call. claude-mem's auto-clear mod did, so Code Mode was off for everyone with claude-mem enabled, even with auto-clear off. The mod now reads tool results through session.append instead. Auto-clear still works on every Claude Code version that runs mods (2.1.287 and later).

Auto-clear picks up where it left off

Auto-clear is still off unless you set CLAUDE_MEM_AUTO_CLEAR_ENABLED to true. When it is on:

  • It resumes with a continuation prompt instead of the bare word "continue" (#6294). Before clearing, Claude saves its work state one last time, naming the list it is working from, the task it just finished and the overall goal. After /clear, auto-clear sends: You are working on "<list>" and recently completed "<task>". Pick back up on our overarching goal "<goal>" from where you left off. If that save left any of the three out, it sends "continue" as before and logs why.
  • It starts at 40% of the context window instead of 33% (#6294). A CLAUDE_MEM_AUTO_CLEAR_PERCENT you set in settings.json or the environment still wins.

New ways to connect

  • Hermes Desktop, search and save over MCP (#6284, thanks @krishnadobhal; part of #5940). claude-mem hermes install --mcp-only --project-cwd <absolute-directory> adds claude-mem as an MCP server for Hermes One and Hermes Desktop without making it the memory provider. You get search and save when you ask for them; nothing is captured or injected on its own. claude-mem hermes uninstall removes only what it wrote and restores config.yaml byte for byte.
  • GitHub Copilot in VS Code (#6286, thanks @krishnadobhal; part of #5895). npx claude-mem install --ide copilot-vscode writes the MCP entry that VS Code's Copilot agent reads (~/.copilot/mcp-config.json, the same file Copilot CLI uses). The entry now includes "tools": ["*"]. Without it, VS Code connected to claude-mem but never offered its tools to the model. No automatic capture yet.
  • ModelGo preset (#6277, thanks @lichloe1116-gif of the ModelGo team). The OpenAI-compatible provider has a modelgo preset for https://api.modelgo.ai/v1. It sets no default model, so pick one your key can use.

Also new

  • Work-state write checks (#6217, thanks @vieGPT). You can make work_state_write run a local check command before it writes to lists you choose, set in ~/.claude-mem/work-state-write-guard.json. A refused write returns HTTP 409 and nothing is saved. With no file, nothing changes.
  • CLAUDE_MEM_API_AGENT_EXTRA_BODY (#6225, thanks @Moep90; #6223). A JSON object added to every request the api agent sends (the My Work refresh and the corpus agent with api). For a Qwen model on vLLM, {"chat_template_kwargs":{"enable_thinking":false}} turns reasoning off. Fields such as model, messages and max_tokens are dropped with a warning, and it is never sent to the cmem.ai gateway.

My Work refresh

  • Slow answers from your own model no longer fail at about 5 minutes (#6224, thanks @Moep90; #6222). Bun's fetch gave up on a connection that had been quiet for 5 minutes, before the api agent's 10-minute request limit applied. Now that limit is the only one.
  • Endpoints that want max_completion_tokens work (#6242). When an endpoint refuses max_tokens, the api agent sends the request once more with max_completion_tokens, instead of failing every call with HTTP 400.
  • A refresh no longer stalls on an answer that was refused twice (#6212, thanks @Moep90). On the one retry for grouping, focus or review, the parts that fail the checks are dropped and the rest is saved. Before, the same batch failed on every run.
  • The build story is written in parts (#6279, thanks @0xABCD01; reported in #6226 by @Moep90). The story step sent every changed review in one question. That question grew past the model's context window, and every refresh after that failed. It now sends parts that fit the same question budget the other steps use.
  • Long conversations are grouped by their latest requests, not their first ten (#6243, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Commits made with git commit --message or --message= are recognized (#6236, thanks @Afloat16).
  • A commit gets a GitHub link only when the evidence names one repository (#6237, thanks @Afloat16).
  • While My Rules waits for an agent, it links the instructions for setting up an API refresh (#6214, thanks @liuyinchu).

Setup, import and export

  • Setup finishes when your prompt history is large (#6280, thanks @0xABCD01; #6266; reported in #6227 by @deserteaglemj). Setup stopped at 99% on "Finding your recent work" when the recent prompts were over the 2 MiB hosted upload limit, and every retry failed the same way. It now finishes setup on your machine and says so. The consent step also shows "Too much for one upload. Untick a tool or skip the scan." and turns off the upload button until the selection fits.
  • Large exports import (#6261, thanks @Afloat16). A file over the worker's 5 MiB request limit was refused (HTTP 413) before anything was saved. Large files are now sent in parts, sessions first, with their full content.
  • Imported observations are found by deduplication the same way live ones are, when CLAUDE_MEM_DEDUP_ENABLED is on (#6263, thanks @Afloat16).
  • claude-mem import --dry-run no longer stops on a bad worker setting, since a preview never calls the worker (#6215, thanks @liuyinchu).
  • Conversations Codex Desktop imported from another agent are no longer counted as prompts typed in Codex (#6219, thanks @rigel905; #6218).
  • Exporting to /dev/stdout, /dev/stderr, a symlink to either, or a FIFO writes clean JSON there, and progress lines and warnings go to the other stream (#6288, from #6258 by @Afloat16).

Fixes

  • Windows: Claude Code loads RULES-AND-TOOLS.md again (#6234, thanks @QuantScorpus). claude-mem wrote its @ import lines with backslashes. Claude Code skips those without a word, and claude-mem then also left out its other copy of the instructions, so neither reached the model. The lines now use forward slashes. An @ import shown inside a fenced code example no longer counts as the real one (#6293, from #6241 by @Afloat16).
  • claude-mem start runs the installed worker (#6235, thanks @ZedingZhang; reported in #6228 by @ExactDoug), not the copy an old shell alias points at, which can be missing its dependencies.
  • Backups keep the new snapshot after the clock is set back (#6273, thanks @Afloat16). Before, cleanup could delete the snapshot it had just reported as saved.
  • teambrain join turns on the new sync connection even when the optional sharing step fails (#6275, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Summaries with no progress in them are not stored (#6202, thanks @ShitK). A summary with only a title or future plans used to be saved and announced as a success.
  • Claude Code's automatic messages no longer split a request (#6251, thanks @Afloat16). The advisor and deliverable scans treated them as new requests and lost earlier advice and file writes from the same turn.
  • Corpus builds over 250,000 observations work (#6271, thanks @Afloat16). They failed with too many SQL variables.
  • Codex: a plugin turned off with a dotted TOML key stays off (#6239, thanks @Afloat16), for example plugins."claude-mem@claude-mem-local".enabled = false.
  • The mem-search skill's frontmatter is valid YAML (#6276, thanks @Afloat16; reported in #6267 by @orbiutyt).
  • Aider: the installer edits the config file Aider actually uses (working directory first, then git root, then home) (#6289, from #6230 by @Afloat16), and keeps quoted file paths intact in read: lists (#6290, from #6231 by @Afloat16).
  • Cursor and Grok context files keep the newer context when two refreshes overlap (#6291, from #6232 by @Afloat16).
  • Self-hosted server: a job with a revoked key or a scope violation runs once instead of three times (#6252), and claude-mem server jobs retry republishes with the queue's retries and backoff (#6253), thanks @HEOJUNFO. The OpenRouter provider reads at most 64 KiB when it checks an answer for a token-field error (#6238, thanks @Afloat16). One unreadable agent event no longer breaks every read of that table (#6199, thanks @DeryFerd).

Security

  • shell-quote 1.11.0 (#6205, thanks @manippoudel; reported in #6200 by @lucasbritolubro). Version 1.9.0 is in the range of CVE-2026-102422 (critical). No code path in claude-mem reached the affected code; this takes it out of what ships.
  • adm-zip 0.6.1 and zod 4.6.7 in the plugin runtime, out of their High advisories (#6220, thanks @LeviReisJs; reported in #6211 by @noumanbhatti).
  • The Gemini API key goes in a header, not the request URL (#6196, thanks @DeryFerd), so proxies and access logs don't record it.
  • Temp files get a random name and are created exclusively at the remaining atomic-write sites (#6198, #6193, thanks @DeryFerd), so a planted symlink can't redirect a write of settings or state files.
  • The self-hosted server's unauthenticated /v1/info shows state only (#6195, thanks @DeryFerd): no auth mode, schema version, Redis host or driver error text.
  • CI: the postinstall allowlist check inspects every direct plugin dependency (#6197), and the spawn environment check reads env blocks declared above a spawn and scans the plugin runtime (#6194), thanks @DeryFerd.

For contributors

  • TypeScript 7.0.2 (#6207, thanks @krishnadobhal; #6188). The scripts and tests that use the compiler API keep TypeScript 6 through an alias.
  • The import smoke test closes its SQLite readers before the final scan (#6216, thanks @liuyinchu).

v15.20.0 Stable2026-11-17T18:46:24Z

Two critical fixes

  • The worker starts when settings.json is not valid JSON (#6259). The worker refused to start when ~/.claude-mem/settings.json couldn't be parsed, even though the database already held the device identity it was trying to save there. Nothing was captured and no context was injected. It now logs a warning, leaves the file untouched, and starts. On a first start with no saved identity, the error names the file and says to repair or delete it.
  • Live Codex Desktop capture on macOS (#6285, thanks @MaxWang007; reported in #6283 by @AlbertValverdeC). Under Bun on macOS, fs.watch gives no event while another process appends to a file it holds open, and Codex Desktop holds a rollout file open for the life of a thread. Codex sessions were read in one burst, sometimes a day later, when Codex closed the file. The transcript watcher now checks the files it already tails every 3 seconds and reads new bytes. It awaits one stat at a time, so a large ~/.codex/sessions history never blocks the worker, and it never opens new files on its own.

Also on main since 15.19.0

Three pieces of the upcoming 16.0.0, already on main, ship in this release too:

  • Mem0 and Supermemory import acceptance fixes (#6206): Supermemory --documents reruns no longer duplicate, and documents imported by 15.19.0 still count as already imported. Mem0 expiry includes its own date. Every import limit names its source; the unsourced 100 MiB total and 10,000-record default are gone (--max-records stays).
  • Classic viewer and TV page redesign (#6229): jumping to the end of a long timeline no longer crashes the viewer (React error 185). A one-row header, timeline kind chips with counts, "Find in loaded memories", day labels, session pages titled by their newest summary, tabbed Settings, and plainer logs. The TV page fits titles in up to three lines and shows a Live dot.
  • Setup step bar and Stop import (#6233): a five-step bar with Back on every screen, errors in plain words with the raw cause under Details, and Stop import (POST /api/setup/import/stop, nothing deleted and nothing restarts by itself), with a receipt of what was added.

v2.3.1 Stable2026-11-18T00:17:57Z

Highlights

  • Timed-Out Shell Commands Keep Running: A shell command still running when its timeout expires now moves to the background instead of being killed, so long builds and test runs finish and report back. A foreground timeout: 0 now means the default 120 seconds. (#63699)
  • Compaction Without the Wait: As a session nears automatic compaction, the summary is now written in the background, so your next prompt doesn't pause for it. Set compaction.background: false to turn it off; a background summary that ends up unused still costs tokens. (#64290)
  • GPT 6.2 with ChatGPT Sign-In: GPT 6.2 Sol and GPT 6.2 Astra, plus their fast variants, now show up in the model picker when you sign in with ChatGPT. (#64508)
  • Update Plugins from Settings: Open Settings › Extensions, click Check for updates, then Update on any plugin with a new version. (#64511)
  • Comment on Selected Code: Selecting code in diff and file viewers now just selects it, so you can copy it, and an Add comment toolbar appears beside the selection. (#64410)

Core

Improvements

  • Shell commands still running when their timeout expires now move to the background instead of being killed, in every build. The shell_timeout_background flag is gone; configs that still set it keep loading. A foreground timeout: 0 now uses the default 120 second timeout instead of waiting until the command exits. (#63699)
  • Background compaction is now on by default: as a session nears automatic compaction, the summary is written in the background so the next prompt doesn't wait for it. Turn it off with compaction.background: false; a background summary that is never used still costs tokens. The experimental.background_compaction setting is removed. (#64290)
  • OpenCode again reads CLAUDE.md in any directory that has no AGENTS.md, and falls back to ~/.claude/CLAUDE.md when the global AGENTS.md is missing. This also applies to instructions found when the agent reads nested files. Set OPENCODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_CODE or OPENCODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_CODE_PROMPT to skip every CLAUDE.md. (#64474, #64533)
  • Added GPT 6.2 Sol and GPT 6.2 Astra, plus their fast variants, to the ChatGPT model list. (#64508)
  • websearch now returns a flat list of results, each with its url, title, content and published date. (#64471)
  • Code Mode and plugins get a simpler structured result from read: text (with offset, lineCount and next), directory (entry names) or media (a URI instead of base64). What the model sees is unchanged. (#64361)
  • Code Mode programs can use Node's Buffer for encoding and decoding bytes. (#64365)
  • In Code Mode, search("issues") now works as shorthand for search({ query: "issues" }). (#64347)
  • Code Mode tool summaries in the catalog can now be up to 500 characters instead of 120, so the model sees more of each tool's description. (#64341)
  • The glob and grep tools now report a malformed filename glob (for example [) as an error that names the parameter to fix, instead of returning no results. (#63960)
  • Server requests that crash now return a 500 with a readable JSON UnknownError body instead of an empty one. Storage problems name the cause (for example "Storage is full" or "Storage is read-only"); other crashes include a ref you can find in the server log. (#64022)
  • The shell tool description now tells the model that its session ID is in the OPENCODE_SESSION_ID environment variable. (#63718)
  • Experimental schedules are now automations, behind the automations flag (the old schedules flag still works). An automation runs on a time, an interval, a cron or a pushed MCP event, and either sends a prompt (to the creating session, another session or a new subagent) or runs a Code Mode program that decides whether to send one. Use /automation or the automation_* tools. Active or paused plain prompt schedules carry over; schedules with a condition or run limit, and finished ones, are dropped. (#64160)

Bug fixes

  • Fixed finished shell commands leaving zombie processes under opencode-pty. The process daemon now cleans each command up when it exits. Background processes started by a successful command (cmd &) keep running, and a failed command's leftovers are stopped. (#64266, #64251)
  • Fixed ChatGPT and OpenAI subscription logins losing their sharing-limit safeguards and context caps when a token refresh failed. (#64470)
  • Fixed Claude Agent models falling back to a 200K context window when Claude Code adds or renames a model. Thanks @Tarquinen. (#64107)
  • Fixed requests being rejected after switching models mid-session on OpenAI-compatible providers. Models that declare a different reasoning field (such as reasoning_content) no longer receive reasoning_details from earlier turns. (#64243)
  • Rejecting a permission request now rejects only the other pending requests from the same step, not every pending request in the session. (#64343)
  • Fixed a finished subagent being reported to its parent as still running when its answer quoted the "Background work still running" heading. (#64026)
  • Fixed ACP clients missing turns that a background shell woke after the original turn ended. (#64262)
  • Reading a small text file now applies the default line and byte limits, so a short file with very many lines is paged instead of returned whole. (#63810)
  • Paged reads that select a single blank line now return that line instead of an empty result. (#63809)
  • read now rejects limit: 0 with "Limit must be a positive integer". (#63808)
  • Text files that start with BM are no longer mistaken for BMP images, and only real GIF87a/GIF89a headers count as GIFs, so these files read as text again. (#63822, #63811)
  • Fixed shell output pages, grep previews, generated titles, read lines and shell output tails splitting emoji and other multi-byte characters, which showed up as �. (#63984, #63967)
  • Shell commands using unknown ANSI-C escapes such as $'\q' are now read the same way Bash and Zsh read them, so permission rules can match them. (#64263)
  • Session title search is now case-insensitive for non-ASCII letters, such as Cyrillic and accented text. (#64259)
  • Fixed grep results being dropped when a matching line or path is not valid UTF-8. Thanks @Tarquinen. (#62249)
  • The @ file picker now finds files in hidden folders such as .github/ and dotfiles such as .env.example when file search uses ripgrep (the default on Windows and outside git repos). .git and gitignored files stay excluded. Thanks @Tarquinen. (#62577)
  • An unreachable well-known integration source is now skipped instead of dropping every well-known source. Thanks @Tarquinen. (#62578)
  • A corrupt cached project ID is now ignored instead of being used. Thanks @Tarquinen. (#62576)
  • Plugin tool renames from tool.execute.before now apply to calls inside Code Mode programs, and a renamed call returns the new tool's structured result. (#63633, #64333)
  • In Code Mode, calling a tool that doesn't exist now returns a rejected promise, so .catch and Promise.allSettled can handle it instead of the whole script failing. (#64510)
  • A Code Mode program that only logs no longer shows a stray null line before its logs. (#64344)

TUI

Improvements

  • Permission prompts from a subagent, and from that subagent's own subagents, now appear while you view the subagent's session. Prompts also show in front of an open composer. (#64343)
  • The export dialog under the new designs can save to the system temp directory as well as the project folder. Thanks @Tarquinen. (#62490)
  • Removed time to first token (ttft) from the assistant message footer. Tokens per second still shows when session.tps is on. (#63759)

Bug fixes

  • When another TUI sharing your tabs closes the session you're viewing, closing it here now moves to the neighbouring tab. (#64261)
  • Fixed broken characters at the edges of the shell output viewer and the inline shell display when output is long. (#63967, #63984)

Desktop

Improvements

  • You can check for and install plugin package updates from Settings › Extensions. Use Check for updates to look for new versions, then click Update on any plugin that has one. (#64511, #64525)
  • Selecting code in diff and file viewers now just selects text, so you can copy it. An Add comment toolbar appears next to the selection to comment on those lines; line numbers and the gutter + still start a comment directly. (#64410)
  • Cmd+F (Ctrl+F) now finds text in Markdown, Mermaid, CSV and HTML previews in the side panel, using the same find bar as the code view. (#63663)
  • Disconnecting a provider or removing an account in Settings → Providers now asks for confirmation first. Disconnecting OpenCode Console says how many providers will stop working. (#64212)
  • The model picker's variant flyout now follows the keyboard: it opens beside the highlighted row, → enters it to pick a variant or toggle Favorite, and ← or Escape goes back. A model's Console budget and reset time now show in the flyout. (#64409, #64423)
  • When New worktree is selected for a new session, the composer shows the base branch (from dev ▾). Click it to pick another base; the choice applies immediately, and Use main checkout switches back. (#64228)
  • Redesigned the folder picker for remote servers, with a path bar, up to three recent-project pills plus a More projects menu, and a search box that shows the selected folder. (#64241)
  • ```latex and ```math code fences now render as display math once complete. (#64380)
  • The new icon set is now the default, and the New icons experiment has been removed. (#64466, #64451)
  • The copy button under assistant messages is now always visible. Message metadata still appears on hover. (#64478)
  • Text files opened in side-panel tabs now have the same copy and download actions as other files. (#64463)
  • Manage models now shows every provider as a card with a ⋯ menu, whether or not you're signed in to OpenCode Console. (#64235)
  • The connect provider dialog has a cleaner layout for every sign-in step: breadcrumbs, method icons, full-width device codes, and a "Signing in from another device?" row for pasting a redirect URL. (#63829)
  • Collapse long messages is no longer experimental. It is now a setting under Timeline preferences. (#64441)
  • Settings now opens on your client preferences, which are listed first, pairing is grouped with Servers, and there's a Help & feedback link. (#64440, #64220)
  • Automatic compactions now always show as a single quiet divider; manual and failed compactions still show the full record. (#64290)
  • Pairing QR codes can carry a server name, which becomes the paired server's default name. (#64139)
  • Image attachment thumbnails are larger, with a 3:2 crop, and a failed image no longer moves the message below it when retried. (#64215)
  • The Hex voice tip no longer appears when Hex is already installed, its copy now says what Hex is, and tips wait an hour after first launch before the first one shows. (#64130, #64131, #63974, #63760)
  • Added missing translations for every non-English language, about 920 strings each. (#64205)

Bug fixes

  • Fixed the desktop app failing to start when drafts.sqlite was a symlink to or copy of the CLI's opencode.db. The desktop now moves that file aside and creates its own, leaving the CLI database untouched. (#64203)
  • Switching session tabs with a keyboard shortcut now focuses the composer. (#64418)
  • Long prompts now stay folded while a new session is being created. (#64452)
  • On macOS, the browser pane stays live while you resize panels instead of showing a still image. (#64443)
  • Submenus now open one at a time and swap without blinking, and stay open while you move the pointer across other items to reach them. (#63739)
  • Pressing a button now dismisses its tooltip until you hover it again, and copy buttons show "Copied" for two seconds, including in the /btw panel. (#63834)
  • Dialogs now use consistent sizes, corner radius and row styles across the command palette, session search, model picker, location picker and other lists. (#63432, #64216, #64206)
  • Sessions on OpenCode Go no longer show a $0.00 cost. (#63744)
  • Use main checkout now stays on one line in the branch picker, and wrapped question descriptions have better spacing. (#64450, #64446)
  • Restyled the Timeline detail slider and visibility buttons in settings so they read as clickable. (#64186)
  • Polished small details: animated counts no longer look faded at rest, the sidebar resize pill is hidden, the sidebar peek insets match, subagent breadcrumbs show a hover background, and screen readers name the redirect URL field after its label only. (#64155, #64219, #64192, #63742, #63885)

SDK

Improvements

  • Unhandled server errors now return a JSON UnknownError body (message and ref) with status 500 instead of an empty body. (#64022)
  • Experimental automation endpoints replace the schedule endpoints: /api/experimental/automation and client.experimental.automation.* (create, list, get, pause, resume, delete). /api/experimental/schedule and client.experimental.schedule.* are removed. (#64160)
  • Config adds compaction.background; experimental.background_compaction and the shell_timeout_background flag are removed. (#64290, #63699)

Bug fixes

  • Apps embedding the SDK with an in-memory database no longer import the user's legacy auth.json credentials. Only a database file opened by path imports them. (#63716)

Extensions

Improvements

  • Each tool call inside a Code Mode program now has its own call ID in plugin hooks and traces. Its permission requests still show on the execute call's row. (#64324)
  • Plugin SessionIdleBackground items can now be { kind: "codemode", sessionID, jobID }, and GUI plugin BridgeLayout accepts an optional clip rectangle. (#64343, #63819)

Bug fixes

  • The browser extension's URL bar action buttons are now spaced 6px apart. (#64214)

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v18.22.2 Stable2026-11-18T01:19:59Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • Added the native Kiro provider: sign in with AWS IAM Identity Center, AWS Builder ID, Google, GitHub, or a Kiro API key (KIRO_API_KEY), stream responses with reasoning, tool calls, and images, and report Kiro credits in /usage (#18955 by @Xytronix, based on #14215 by @ajdiyassin)
  • Added a definitive option to OAuthError; isDefinitiveOAuthFailure now takes the error object and disables a credential marked definitive without matching its message (#18955 by @Xytronix)
  • buildAnthropicSearchHeaders takes extraBetas, request-specific betas sent beside the web search beta (#18125 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added omitDemotedReasoning to StreamOptions: an Anthropic request leaves out other models' reasoning that it would otherwise replay as text, and a refused request reports how many such blocks it carried in demotedReasoningBlocks (#10407) (#17610 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed shutting down or cancelling an omp session making every other session sharing the agent directory see its OAuth accounts as depleted for 5 minutes; still-valid tokens are also served during shutdown (#19607)
  • OAuth accounts refreshed through an auth broker keep their stored routing metadata (such as an API endpoint) in the key built for the request, instead of losing it after the first refresh (#18955 by @Xytronix, based on #14215 by @ajdiyassin)
  • Fenced (```thinking) reasoning with very long unbroken lines no longer slows down as it streams (#19493 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed Anthropic requests that replay a signed server-compaction block still asking for a message thread, which the API refuses (thread_unsupported_request), so every such request first paid a rejected round trip before resending without the thread (#19473 by @alphastorm)
  • Anthropic requests whose connection drops before any response now say how long after the request started it dropped and whether the request body was still uploading or had all been written to the socket, with its size, instead of a bare "Connection error." (#18624 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Anthropic requests dropping the reasoning of earlier non-Claude turns signed in under another credential, or kept through a compaction; it is now replayed like other models' reasoning (#7241) (#17610 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added Kiro to the model catalog with automatic discovery of every model available to the signed-in Kiro account (#18955 by @Xytronix, based on #14215 by @ajdiyassin)
  • Added Factory Droid's Claude Fable 5.2, Gemini 4 Carbon (with its launch credit promotion, half the input rate until 2026-12-01) and Kimi K3.1 Pro, each listed once the account's feature flag enables it, and GPT Image 2.6 and GPT Image 2.6 Flare ahead of GPT Image 2.5 in the image role's Factory defaults (#19191 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added writeModelCacheAsync() and forEachInSlices(): model refreshes now build and cache discovered models in slices that yield to the event loop; writeModelCache() returns its outcome and no longer rewrites a provider whose rows are unchanged

Changed

  • readModelCache() parses a payload once per process: cache files holding identical rows return the same shared row objects

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • Computer el.setValue(75) now writes the number as "75" instead of refusing a non-string; NaN, Infinity, booleans and objects are still refused, as are a whole number past 2^53 and a number that prints with an exponent, which are passed as text in digits instead (#19405 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added a report of native windows to browser results on macOS for visible managed browsers, spawned Chromium browsers and a loopback relay: a <select> menu, colour or date picker, or a print, save, sign-in, passkey or permission sheet or bubble a call raised over its tab is named with its computer.window() id while the browser's tab list shows that tab in front of its window, by the next call when it shows just after the call returned, and on failed calls too; connected CDP endpoints, remote relays and page-backed popup windows are excluded (#18925 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added Kiro as a built-in provider: run /login kiro, pick Identity Center, Builder ID, or an API key, and choose a discovered kiro/<model-id> model. Each /login kiro adds an account (#18955 by @Xytronix, based on #14215 by @ajdiyassin)
  • Added Google and GitHub to /login kiro: approve a one-time code at app.kiro.dev, then sign in with that account (#18955 by @Xytronix, based on #14215 by @ajdiyassin)
  • /usage and omp usage now show Kiro credits: the monthly plan credits with their reset date, active bonus credits with their expiry, and overage credits and charges when overage is enabled (#18955 by @Xytronix, based on #14215 by @ajdiyassin)

Changed

  • Model registries in one process (SDK embedders, tests) now share discovered-model builds, so refreshing another registry skips rebuilding models an earlier one already built

Fixed

  • Fixed the UI freezing briefly when a prompt is submitted: prompt history is now written to disk in the background (within 100 ms, and on exit) instead of on the Enter keystroke
  • Fixed the UI freezing while discovered models from large provider catalogs are merged: the merge now runs in slices that yield to the event loop
  • Fixed closing omp's headless browser taking about 7 s when Chromium never finishes a graceful shutdown (seen with Chrome for Testing 150 on macOS 27): a browser on an omp-created profile is now killed at once
  • Failed computer runs retain text and screenshots already produced in JavaScript and Python Eval, so agents can inspect partial actions without losing the error; direct computer calls also show the failure's name and message alongside partial output (#19100 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Exa and Firecrawl web searches silently falling back to their keyless public endpoints when a configured credential failed to resolve (for example a failing !command); they now report the credential error
  • Fixed eval cells being able to call yield (directly or through xd://yield) without finishing the delegated task; eval now rejects yield and points to the direct tool (#19470 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed service ready.log and wait patterns not matching ^ and $ at line boundaries after earlier output (#19471 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the shell's stat, diff and find builtins ignoring TZ when printing file times; find -ls now prints times in the shell's time zone instead of UTC, and find -newermt reads dates like Nov 15, 2023 in the shell's time zone (#19482 by @alphastorm)
  • With TTSR rules active, large write and edit calls no longer slow down as their arguments stream (#19494 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed /commit and eval commit creating a single-parent commit during a merge; committing now refuses while a merge, cherry-pick, revert, rebase or git am is in progress or the index has unresolved conflicts (#19474 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the apply_patch tool description calling it a shell command and forbidding absolute paths (#19325, #19468 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the shell's rg builtin ignoring --glob patterns that contain a slash (#19476 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed eval commit only(...) failing when one of its paths or globs matched no changed file; unmatched ones are now reported (#19472 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed a Tern browser block answering every later browser call with "Tab is busy: another call on this tab is still running" after a call whose page evaluation never returned, such as one cut off by a redirect; such a call now ends at its time limit or when cancelled, and the tab takes the next call
  • Fixed the browser tool reporting a JavaScript confirm or prompt as still open, and failing page calls on it, after the user clicked it away or another CDP client answered it (#19481 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed computer setValue on a <select> in Chrome or an Electron app on macOS failing with an empty list of options; it now chooses the option the value names (#19224 by @will-bogusz)
  • A relay tab is no longer closed when another extension's frame, such as a password manager's autofill menu that undoes the relay clearing it, keeps Chrome refusing omp: the call fails saying what holds the tab, the tab stays open as the user left it, and the next call reattaches to it (#19178 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed the computer tool telling the model that only an X11 takeover can end with nothing sent because the user kept typing; a macOS takeover now waits for the user's pause too and reports UserIntervened the same way (#19296 by @will-bogusz)
  • When the browser relay hides another extension's frame in a tab omp drives (a password manager's inline menu or save card), the call that ran into it, or a later one on that tab, now names the frame's page, the extension's id and the page's own title, and says the frame is hidden for the user too (#19001 by @will-bogusz)
  • Anthropic web search now passes site:-scoped queries to Claude with the domain and path still in the text, and tells Claude to always search, so those lookups and queries it thought it could answer from memory no longer fail with "returned no sources" (#18125 by @will-bogusz)
  • Anthropic web search on models with a refusal fallback (Opus 5 and 5.5, Sonnet 5.5, Fable 5) has Anthropic retry a query the safety classifier declines on another Claude model, and reports a refusal or a failed search (too_many_requests, …) by name instead of "returned no sources" (#18125 by @will-bogusz)
  • Subagents without an output schema (including eval agent() calls without schema) no longer get a yield tool; their final message is the result.
  • A yield without data no longer uses the subagent's last message as the result; it only finalizes incremental sections that were already submitted.
  • A computer key press that opens a macOS popup button's or menu button's menu in the background now fails listing the items and naming the press or click that chooses one, instead of leaving the menu open with your keyboard (#19179 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Claude not seeing the reasoning of earlier Codex, Kimi, Grok and other non-Claude turns after a model switch, including turns kept through a compaction; it now gets that reasoning as text. When Claude refuses a turn with Refusal (reasoning_extraction) because of that carried reasoning, the turn is retried once without it, and the session stops sending it to Claude (#7241) (#17610 by @will-bogusz)
  • After an Anthropic refusal, later turns no longer resend the refusal text with the tool calls it stopped: those calls now replay as "not executed" only, so one reasoning_extraction refusal stops making every following turn of the session likely to be refused too (#19419 by @flosrn)
  • Fixed selective commits skipping updated submodule pointers; existing initialized gitlinks can now be reviewed and explicitly selected without committing changes inside the submodule (#19556 by @kim-dali)
  • Fixed git reads such as /commit's status scan hanging when a background git process kept the output pipe open (#19478 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the browser falling back to Chromium on the first launch of a new WebKit engine build on macOS: the WebKit host now gets 60 seconds instead of 10 to answer its greeting while macOS scans the engine's binaries and libraries (#19423 by @shgew)
  • Fixed --continue opening a new empty session when the terminal's last session was started in another directory and closed before its first message; it now resumes the current directory's newest session (#19495).
  • Fixed a rejecting pre-commit hook's stdout (e.g. the pre-commit framework's per-hook report) missing from the error raised by the eval commit namespace and omp commit (#19449)
  • /commit now shows the subject a commit-msg hook wrote onto the commit, instead of the subject from before the hook ran (#19448 by @eggpeat)
  • ^model references followed by sentence punctuation now register their model agent instead of remaining literal (#19445 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Selective commits in sparse checkouts no longer report paths outside the cone as unstaged deletions to commit hooks (#19453 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Custom providers pointing at the Zhipu Coding Plan endpoint now retry at the Beijing-time quota reset instead of waiting eight hours too long (#19446 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • /copy now copies what a mid-turn out:// reply showed, instead of the reply tool's own "Shown to the user." result, and lists the reply's code blocks in the turn's blocks (#19551 by @bannert1337)
  • Fixed tools enabled during goal mode becoming unavailable after leaving it (#19514 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Fixed the status-line usage segment showing another account's quota for multi-credential API-key providers such as opencode-go; it now follows the credential row the session router actually serves from (#19456) (#19492 by @thekitaev).
  • Fixed Objective-C message results and arm64 retain-register data flow in Ghidra analysis of Mach-O binaries, including previously analyzed cache-extracted frameworks (#19558 by @shgew).
  • With a modal dialog open, browser fill, type and press aimed at a field behind it now fail naming what covers it, as click does, instead of typing into the dialog's button and reporting success (on Chromium tabs and Tern tabs without native DOM reads); press also refuses a disabled element (on Chromium tabs and Tern tabs without native DOM reads), and Chromium drag refuses a covered start or end (#19524 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed RPC login refusing providers with an at-your-own-risk warning (antigravity-cli, google-antigravity): the warning now reaches the host as an extension_ui_request input, and login continues on "yes" and cancels on anything else (#19523)
  • On Linux, file watching for language servers no longer puts an inotify watch on every directory under node_modules and .git/objects, which could use up the system's inotify watch limit and keep other programs such as systemd from adding watches (#19459)
  • /restart now re-execs through the launcher omp was started by, so an open session moves onto an upgraded binary instead of re-running a retained old install (#19529 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • RPC hosts now always receive a terminal agent_end after an over-quota 429: usage-limit bookkeeping is bounded so a stalled usage report can no longer hold the event (#19411, #19417 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Rex and other OSC 7501 terminals no longer play a success sound mid-run when a stale agent end arrives after another run has opened (#17307, #19418 by @yuzu-octopus)

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Added

  • TtyWriter now works on Windows consoles: it writes off the JS thread in 16 KiB WriteFile chunks, restores the UTF-8 console code pages that child processes change, and stop() cancels a blocked write
  • Added isolateThreadStartsFromConsole(), which keeps a Windows console whose terminal stopped reading from blocking new threads in the process

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • ui.loop-blocked log records now name what held the event loop (cause, causeShare, top), sampled by an always-on profiler; PI_LOOP_SAMPLER=0 turns it off

Changed

  • On Windows, terminal output is written off the JS thread, so input and rendering stay responsive while the console drains a large repaint

Fixed

  • On Windows, omp no longer freezes when the terminal stops reading its output (a hung Windows Terminal, a paused ssh client); it keeps running and catches up once the terminal reads again

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Added

  • Added readBoundedBody and readBoundedJson for reading HTTP response bodies with a size limit, with an optional signal so a stalled peer cannot outlive the caller's deadline (#18955 by @Xytronix)

What's Changed

  • feat: native Kiro provider (IdC, Builder ID, Google/GitHub sign-in, /usage) by @Xytronix in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18955
  • fix(computer): retain output and screenshots when a run fails by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/19100
